   400	                read_strand = "+" if rd.get("is_forward") else "-"
   401	                if read_strand != interval.strand:
   402	                    continue
   403	            read_id = rd.get("query_name")
   404	            if read_id:
   405	                all_read_ids.add(read_id)
   406	                # Capture primary-alignment sequence only — scope is this
   407	                # interval's fetch, satisfying the per-interval read-set
   408	                # constraint. Used by R1 mappy-argmax bypass.
   409	                if not rd.get("is_secondary") and not rd.get("is_supplementary"):
   410	                    seq = rd.get("query_sequence")
   411	                    if seq:
   412	                        read_sequences.setdefault(read_id, seq)
   413	            if is_fusion_read(rd):
   414	                continue
   415	            non_fusion_reads.append(rd)
   416	
   417	    if not non_fusion_reads:
   418	        return CandidateSet(
   419	            interval=interval,
   420	            candidates=[],
   421	            read_ids=all_read_ids,
   422	            read_sequences=read_sequences,
   423	        )
   424	
   425	    # Determine strand from reads
   426	    strand = interval.strand or "+"
   427	
   428	    # NEW generation-side clustering (production default). Group reads by intron chain
   429	    # (3' ignored), collapse EXACT sub-chains, cluster wobble/cassette/containment
   430	    # keeping members; NO canonical-search shadow generation. Bypasses the legacy
   431	    # 3'+exact-chain grouping and canonical expansion entirely.
   432	    if chain_cluster:
   433	        return _chain_cluster_candidates(
   434	            interval, non_fusion_reads, all_read_ids, read_sequences,
   435	            gtf_reader, genome_fasta, strand, min_novel_reads,
   436	            wobble_bp=chain_cluster_wobble_bp,
   437	            cassette_max_exon_bp=chain_cluster_cassette_max_exon_bp,
   438	            fold_monoexon_contained=chain_cluster_fold_monoexon,
   439	            fold_span_guard=chain_cluster_fold_span_guard,
   440	        )
   441	
   442	    # 2. Group reads by 3' + intron chain → novel candidate groups.
   443	    # Optional pre-processing:
   444	    #   - canonical_search_bp>0: for each junction, scan the genome ±N bp for
   445	    #     canonical GT-AG motifs and emit the resulting chain alternatives
   446	    #     alongside the original (recovering ambiguous-GT-AG strict misses
   447	    #     where multiple valid splice sites exist within a few bp).
   448	    multi_chain_override = None
   449	
   450	    if canonical_search_bp > 0:
   451	        # Pre-extract CIGAR-derived chains once.
   452	        read_chains = []
   453	        for rd in non_fusion_reads:
   454	            cigar = rd.get("cigartuples")
   455	            ref_start = rd.get("reference_start")
   456	            if cigar is None or ref_start is None:
   457	                continue
   458	            read_chains.append((rd, extract_intron_chain(cigar, ref_start)))
   459	
   460	        multi_chain_override = expand_canonical_chain_alternatives_v2(
   461	            read_chains, genome_fasta, strand,
   462	            motif_set=parse_motifs(canonical_motifs),
   463	            search_bp=canonical_search_bp,
   464	            max_chains_per_read=max_chains_per_read,
   465	        )
   466	
   467	    if denovo_graph:
   468	        # Intron-graph assembly: group reads by their EXTENDED (assembled) chain
   469	        # so truncated partials become one full-length candidate. Takes precedence
   470	        # over canonical-search expansion.
   471	        groups = _graph_groups(
   472	            non_fusion_reads, strand, denovo_graph_tol, denovo_graph_min_edge_reads,
   473	            tss_brake=denovo_graph_tss_brake, tss_tol=denovo_graph_tss_tol,
   474	            tss_min_reads=denovo_graph_tss_min_reads, tss_frac=denovo_graph_tss_frac,
   475	        )
   476	    else:
   477	        groups = group_reads_by_three_prime_and_intron_chain(
   478	            non_fusion_reads, strand, threshold=threshold,
   479	            multi_chain_override=multi_chain_override,
   480	        )
   481	
   482	    # 3. Get GTF transcripts in region
   483	    gtf_transcripts = []
   484	    if gtf_reader is not None:
   485	        gtf_transcripts = gtf_reader.get_transcripts_in_region(
   486	            interval.chrom, interval.start, interval.end
   487	        )
   488	
   489	    # 4. Build GTF candidates with intron chains
   490	    gtf_candidates = []
   491	    for tx in gtf_transcripts:
   492	        # Strand-separated interval: skip opposite-strand annotation (mirror of the
   493	        # chain-cluster path). No effect when the interval is unstranded / no GTF.
   494	        if interval.strand is not None and tx.strand != interval.strand:
   495	            continue
   496	        chain = gtf_transcript_to_intron_chain(tx)
   497	        tx.sort_features()
   498	        # Get 3' position from GTF
   499	        if tx.strand == "+":
   500	            three_prime = tx.end
   501	        else:
   502	            three_prime = tx.start
   503	
   504	        seq = tx.get_spliced_sequence(genome_fasta)
   505	
   506	        gtf_candidates.append(
   507	            TranscriptCandidate(
   508	                candidate_id=tx.transcript_id,
   509	                intron_chain=chain,
   510	                three_prime_pos=three_prime,
   511	                sequence=seq,
   512	                source="gtf",
   513	                supporting_read_ids=set(),
   514	                chrom=interval.chrom,
   515	                strand=tx.strand,
   516	                start=tx.start,
   517	                end=tx.end,
   518	            )
   519	        )
   520	
   521	    # 5. Match novel groups vs GTF: same intron chain + 3' within threshold → merge
   522	    candidates: List[TranscriptCandidate] = list(gtf_candidates)
   523	    matched_group_keys: Set = set()
   524	
   525	    for group_key, reads in groups.items():
   526	        consensus_3prime, chain = group_key
   527	        read_ids_in_group = {rd["query_name"] for rd in reads if "query_name" in rd}
   528	
   529	        # Try to match with a GTF candidate
   530	        matched = False
   531	        for gtf_cand in gtf_candidates:
   532	            if _intron_chains_match(chain, gtf_cand.intron_chain) and _three_prime_within_threshold(
   533	                consensus_3prime, gtf_cand.three_prime_pos, threshold
   534	            ):
   535	                gtf_cand.supporting_read_ids.update(read_ids_in_group)
   536	                matched = True
   537	                matched_group_keys.add(group_key)
   538	                break
   539	
   540	        if not matched:
   541	            # Novel candidate: 5' takes longest read in the group; the spliced
   542	            # cDNA sequence is stitched from genome FASTA + the intron chain.
   543	            rep = pick_representative_read(reads)
   544	            if denovo_graph:
   545	                # Assembled candidate spans regions no single read covers -> use
   546	                # the UNION of the group's reads' extents so the genome-stitched
   547	                # spliced sequence covers the full extended intron chain.
   548	                starts = [rd.get("reference_start") for rd in reads
   549	                          if rd.get("reference_start") is not None]
   550	                ends = [rd.get("reference_end") for rd in reads
   551	                        if rd.get("reference_end") is not None]
   552	                ref_start = min(starts) if starts else interval.start
   553	                ref_end = max(ends) if ends else interval.end
   554	            else:
   555	                ref_start = rep.get("reference_start", interval.start)
   556	                ref_end = rep.get("reference_end", interval.end)
   557	
   558	            spliced_seq = _build_spliced_sequence(
   559	                genome_fasta, ref_start, ref_end, chain, strand
   560	            )
   561	            if not spliced_seq:
   562	                logger.warning(
   563	                    "Skipping novel candidate at %s:%d-%d (%s): empty spliced "
   564	                    "sequence from genome FASTA (chrom_len=%d).",
   565	                    interval.chrom,
   566	                    ref_start,
   567	                    ref_end,
   568	                    strand,
   569	                    len(genome_fasta) if genome_fasta else 0,
   570	                )
   571	                continue
   572	
   573	            candidates.append(
   574	                TranscriptCandidate(
   575	                    candidate_id=_generate_novel_id(),
   576	                    intron_chain=chain,
   577	                    three_prime_pos=consensus_3prime,
   578	                    sequence=spliced_seq,
   579	                    source="novel",
   580	                    supporting_read_ids=read_ids_in_group,
   581	                    chrom=interval.chrom,
   582	                    strand=strand,
   583	                    start=ref_start,
   584	                    end=ref_end,
   585	                )
   586	            )
   587	
   588	    # 6. Collapse: novel candidates with same intron chain + nearby 3' end
   589	    collapsed = _collapse_candidates(
   590	        candidates, threshold, wobble_tol=denovo_wobble_tol,
   591	        wobble_shadow_ratio=denovo_wobble_shadow_ratio)
   592	
   593	    # 7. (A2) Drop novel candidates below min_novel_reads threshold. GTF and
   594	    # fusion candidates are not affected by this filter.
   595	    if min_novel_reads > 1:
   596	        before = len(collapsed)
   597	        collapsed = [
   598	            c
   599	            for c in collapsed
   600	            if c.source != "novel" or len(c.supporting_read_ids) >= min_novel_reads
   601	        ]
   602	        dropped = before - len(collapsed)
   603	        if dropped:
   604	            logger.info(
   605	                "Interval %s: dropped %d novel candidates with <%d supporting reads",
   606	                interval.region_string,
   607	                dropped,
   608	                min_novel_reads,
   609	            )
   610	
   611	    logger.info(
   612	        "Interval %s: %d candidates (%d GTF, %d novel) from %d reads",
   613	        interval.region_string,
   614	        len(collapsed),
   615	        sum(1 for c in collapsed if c.source == "gtf"),
   616	        sum(1 for c in collapsed if c.source == "novel"),
   617	        len(all_read_ids),
   618	    )
   619	
   620	    return CandidateSet(
   621	        interval=interval,
   622	        candidates=collapsed,
   623	        read_ids=all_read_ids,
   624	        read_sequences=read_sequences,
   625	    )
   626	
   627	
   628	def merge_fusion_candidates(
   629	    candidate_set: CandidateSet,
   630	    fusion_candidates: List[TranscriptCandidate],
   631	) -> CandidateSet:
   632	    """Append fusion candidates to an existing CandidateSet, deduplicating by ID.
   633	
   634	    Args:
   635	        candidate_set: Existing set of candidates (GTF/novel). Mutated in place.
   636	        fusion_candidates: Fusion candidates to merge in.
   637	
   638	    Returns:
   639	        The same CandidateSet instance with fusion candidates appended.
   640	    """
   641	    existing_ids: Set[str] = {c.candidate_id for c in candidate_set.candidates}
   642	    for fc in fusion_candidates:
   643	        if fc.candidate_id not in existing_ids:
   644	            candidate_set.candidates.append(fc)
   645	            existing_ids.add(fc.candidate_id)
   646	
   647	    all_ids = [c.candidate_id for c in candidate_set.candidates]
   648	    if len(all_ids) != len(set(all_ids)):
   649	        raise ValueError("Duplicate candidate_ids found after merge")
   650	
   651	    return candidate_set
   652	
   653	
   654	def _wobble_chain_match(a, b, tol: int) -> bool:
   655	    """True iff two intron chains have the SAME number of introns and every
   656	    corresponding (donor, acceptor) is within ``tol`` bp — same structure up to
   657	    alignment wobble."""
   658	    if a.num_introns != b.num_introns:
   659	        return False
   660	    for (s1, e1), (s2, e2) in zip(a.introns, b.introns):
   661	        if abs(s1 - s2) > tol or abs(e1 - e2) > tol:
   662	            return False
   663	    return True
   664	
   665	
   666	def _wobble_collapse_novel(
   667	    novel: List[TranscriptCandidate], threshold: int, tol: int,
   668	    shadow_ratio: float = 1.0,
   669	) -> List[TranscriptCandidate]:
   670	    """Greedy consensus merge of novel candidates that differ only by junction
   671	    wobble (each junction within ``tol`` bp) AND share a 3' end within
   672	    ``threshold``. The HIGHEST-read-support candidate is the consensus
   673	    representative (the reads' own mode); wobbled shadows union their reads into
   674	    it and vanish. Deterministic order: (-support, -length, start, 3', end, chain).
   675	
   676	    ``shadow_ratio`` guards against merging genuine CLOSE isoforms (real
   677	    alternative donor/acceptor sites a few bp apart, e.g. NAGNAG): a candidate is
   678	    absorbed into a rep only if it is a true SHADOW — ``len(cand.reads) <=
   679	    shadow_ratio * len(rep.reads)`` (rep always has >= support, given the sort).
   680	    A minimap wobble shadow has few reads; a real close isoform has comparable
   681	    support and stays separate. isoquant's bulge-removal uses the ½ analogue.
   682	    ``shadow_ratio >= 1.0`` merges any wobble-match (no isoform protection).
   683	
   684	    NOT annotation-snap: the consensus coordinate comes from the most-supported
   685	    READS, never a reference. Merges shadows BEFORE they exist as separate
   686	    candidates (reads never split-then-dropped — avoids the read-reassignment
   687	    trap that made the post-hoc dominance filter backfire).
   688	    """
   689	    # Total, data-derived order (no reliance on input order or the id counter):
   690	    # highest read support first (= consensus), then longest, then genomic
   691	    # coords + chain. This makes both the representative choice AND the greedy
   692	    # clustering deterministic regardless of the candidates' input order.
   693	    # Total order (candidate_id last makes it strict, so representative choice
   694	    # and clustering are fully input-order-independent).
   695	    order = sorted(
   696	        novel,
   697	        key=lambda c: (-len(c.supporting_read_ids), -c.length, c.start,
   698	                       c.three_prime_pos, c.end, tuple(c.intron_chain.introns),
   699	                       c.candidate_id),
   700	    )
   701	    reps: List[TranscriptCandidate] = []
   702	    for cand in order:
   703	        merged = False
   704	        for rep in reps:
   705	            if (abs(rep.three_prime_pos - cand.three_prime_pos) > threshold
   706	                    or not _wobble_chain_match(rep.intron_chain, cand.intron_chain, tol)):
   707	                continue
   708	            # EXACT-chain matches always merge (superset of the disabled exact
   709	            # path). The shadow_ratio guard applies ONLY to inexact (wobbled)
   710	            # matches — it protects genuine close isoforms, not exact duplicates.
   711	            exact = rep.intron_chain == cand.intron_chain
   712	            if exact or (len(cand.supporting_read_ids)
   713	                         <= shadow_ratio * len(rep.supporting_read_ids)):
   714	                rep.supporting_read_ids.update(cand.supporting_read_ids)
   715	                merged = True
   716	                break
   717	        if not merged:
   718	            reps.append(cand)
   719	    return reps
   720	
   721	
   722	def _exact_collapse_novel(
   723	    novel: List[TranscriptCandidate], threshold: int
   724	) -> List[TranscriptCandidate]:
   725	    """Exact-chain collapse (THE default path — byte-identical when wobble is
   726	    off). Bucket novel candidates by EXACT intron chain, then within each bucket
   727	    greedily merge candidates whose 3' ends are within ``threshold`` of an
   728	    existing (longest-span) representative, unioning supporting reads."""
   729	    novel_by_chain: dict = {}
   730	    for cand in novel:
   731	        novel_by_chain.setdefault(cand.intron_chain, []).append(cand)
   732	    collapsed: List[TranscriptCandidate] = []
   733	    for chain, bucket in novel_by_chain.items():
   734	        # Sort by length desc so the longest-span candidate is the representative
   735	        # of its 3' cluster (avoiding "first-wins" arbitrariness).
   736	        bucket.sort(key=lambda c: c.length, reverse=True)
   737	        reps: List[TranscriptCandidate] = []
   738	        for cand in bucket:
   739	            merged = False
   740	            for rep in reps:
   741	                if abs(rep.three_prime_pos - cand.three_prime_pos) <= threshold:
   742	                    # rep is already at least as long (sort order). Only union the
   743	                    # supporting reads; do NOT extend rep.start/rep.end (rep.sequence
   744	                    # was built from rep's own span; coords/sequence must stay
   745	                    # consistent — #P2-3).
   746	                    rep.supporting_read_ids.update(cand.supporting_read_ids)
   747	                    merged = True
   748	                    break
   749	            if not merged:
   750	                reps.append(cand)
   751	        collapsed.extend(reps)
   752	    return collapsed
   753	
   754	
   755	def _collapse_candidates(
   756	    candidates: List[TranscriptCandidate], threshold: int, wobble_tol: int = 0,
   757	    wobble_shadow_ratio: float = 1.0,
   758	) -> List[TranscriptCandidate]:
   759	    """Collapse novel candidates with same intron chain and nearby 3' end.
   760	
   761	    The exact-chain collapse (:func:`_exact_collapse_novel`) ALWAYS runs. When
   762	    ``wobble_tol`` > 0, its reps are then wobble-collapsed
   763	    (:func:`_wobble_collapse_novel`) — so wobble-on is a STRICT SUPERSET of the
   764	    exact collapse (exact 3'-chaining fully preserved; wobble only adds inexact
   765	    shadow absorption). ``wobble_tol == 0`` is byte-identical (exact only).
   766	
   767	    Two-pass algorithm (A1):
   768	      Pass 1: bucket NOVEL candidates by exact intron chain.
   769	      Pass 2: within each chain-bucket, greedily merge candidates whose 3'
   770	              ends fall within ``threshold`` of an existing representative.
   771	    The representative is always the longest-span candidate (5' extension);
   772	    supporting read IDs are unioned.
   773	
   774	    GTF candidates are passed through unchanged (annotation-level duplicates
   775	    are preserved so ground-truth recall metrics stay interpretable).
   776	    Fusion candidates are also passed through unchanged.
   777	    """
   778	    if not candidates:
   779	        return []
   780	
   781	    gtf_passthrough: List[TranscriptCandidate] = []
   782	    fusion_candidates: List[TranscriptCandidate] = []
   783	    novel: List[TranscriptCandidate] = []
   784	
   785	    for cand in candidates:
   786	        if cand.source == "fusion":
   787	            fusion_candidates.append(cand)
   788	            continue
   789	        if cand.source == "gtf":
   790	            gtf_passthrough.append(cand)
   791	            continue
   792	        novel.append(cand)
   793	
   794	    # Exact collapse ALWAYS (byte-identical to the disabled path); wobble on top.
   795	    collapsed_novel = _exact_collapse_novel(novel, threshold)
   796	    if wobble_tol > 0:
   797	        collapsed_novel = _wobble_collapse_novel(
   798	            collapsed_novel, threshold, wobble_tol, wobble_shadow_ratio)
   799	
   800	    return gtf_passthrough + collapsed_novel + fusion_candidates
===== fin/candidates/junction_graph.py =====
     1	"""De-novo intron graph: assemble full-length transcript intron chains from
     2	partial/truncated read chains.
     3	
     4	Measured motivation (p00, no annotation): pyfin's dominant de-novo error is
     5	truncation — candidates that are a contiguous sub-chain of a real transcript
     6	(gffcompare class 'c', ~16% of output), because dRNA reads are 3'-biased so a
     7	read covers only the 3' junctions. pyfin currently emits each read's exact chain
     8	as a candidate, so a truncated read -> a truncated candidate.
     9	
    10	This module pools all reads' junctions, clusters wobbled junctions to a
    11	read-count consensus, builds a directed read-adjacency graph (nodes = consensus
    12	junctions ordered by genomic coord; edges = "these two junctions are consecutive
    13	in >= min_edge_reads reads"), and EXTENDS each read's chain through
    14	UNAMBIGUOUSLY-supported edges to a maximal chain. Extension stops at genuine
    15	branch points (a node with >1 supported successor), so it lengthens truncated
    16	chains WITHOUT fabricating wrong junction combinations.
    17	
    18	NOT annotation-snap: consensus coordinates and edges come only from reads.
    19	"""
    20	from __future__ import annotations
    21	
    22	from collections import Counter, defaultdict
    23	from typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple
    24	
    25	Junction = Tuple[int, int]
    26	Chain = Tuple[Junction, ...]
    27	
    28	
    29	def cluster_junctions(chains: List[Chain], tol: int) -> Dict[Junction, Junction]:
    30	    """Map every observed junction to a consensus junction. Greedy by read count:
    31	    the most-observed junction seeds a centroid; a junction within ``tol`` bp on
    32	    BOTH donor and acceptor maps to the first matching centroid. Deterministic
    33	    (count desc, then coordinate)."""
    34	    counts: Counter = Counter()
    35	    for ch in chains:
    36	        for j in ch:
    37	            counts[j] += 1
    38	    consensus: Dict[Junction, Junction] = {}
    39	    centroids: List[Junction] = []
    40	    for j in sorted(counts, key=lambda x: (-counts[x], x)):
    41	        hit = None
    42	        for cj in centroids:
    43	            if abs(j[0] - cj[0]) <= tol and abs(j[1] - cj[1]) <= tol:
    44	                hit = cj
    45	                break
    46	        if hit is None:
    47	            centroids.append(j)
    48	            consensus[j] = j
    49	        else:
    50	            consensus[j] = hit
    51	    return consensus
    52	
    53	
    54	def build_graph(cons_chains: List[Chain]) -> Tuple[Counter, Counter]:
    55	    """Node + edge read-support counts from consensus-mapped chains.
    56	    Edge (a, b) = a and b are consecutive junctions in a read's chain."""
    57	    node_counts: Counter = Counter()
    58	    edge_counts: Counter = Counter()
    59	    for ch in cons_chains:
    60	        for j in ch:
    61	            node_counts[j] += 1
    62	        for a, b in zip(ch, ch[1:]):
    63	            edge_counts[(a, b)] += 1
    64	    return node_counts, edge_counts
    65	
    66	
    67	def anchored_5p_junctions(
    68	    cons_chains: List[Chain],
    69	    five_prime_pos: List[int],
    70	    strand: str,
    71	    tss_tol: int,
    72	    min_tss_reads: int,
    73	    tss_frac: float,
    74	) -> Set[Junction]:
    75	    """Return the set of 5'-terminal consensus junctions that sit at a real TSS.
    76	
    77	    dRNA is 3'-biased: a truncated read of a LONG transcript stops at a RANDOM 5'
    78	    position, but a COMPLETE read of a genuine short isoform stops at that
    79	    isoform's real TSS. So among the reads whose 5'-most junction is ``J`` (they
    80	    begin at ``J``), if a strong fraction pile their read-5'-ends within
    81	    ``tss_tol`` bp of one position, ``J`` marks a real transcription start and a
    82	    chain ending there must NOT be extended further 5'-ward (that would erase the
    83	    short isoform). Empirically (p00) such peaks carry 40-90% of a locus's reads;
    84	    degradation-only starts scatter and never cross ``tss_frac``.
    85	
    86	    5'-most junction is ``chain[0]`` on '+' (5' = small coord) and ``chain[-1]``
    87	    on '-' (5' = large coord).
    88	    """
    89	    by_term: Dict[Junction, List[int]] = defaultdict(list)
    90	    for cc, p in zip(cons_chains, five_prime_pos):
    91	        if not cc or p is None:
    92	            continue
    93	        term = cc[0] if strand == "+" else cc[-1]
    94	        by_term[term].append(p)
    95	    anchored: Set[Junction] = set()
    96	    for term, positions in by_term.items():
    97	        if len(positions) < min_tss_reads:
    98	            continue
    99	        best = max(sum(1 for q in positions if abs(q - p) <= tss_tol)
   100	                   for p in positions)
   101	        if best >= min_tss_reads and best >= tss_frac * len(positions):
   102	            anchored.add(term)
   103	    return anchored
   104	
   105	
   106	def assemble_chains(
   107	    chains: List[Chain],
   108	    tol: int,
   109	    min_edge_reads: int,
   110	    five_prime_pos: List[int] = None,
   111	    strand: str = "+",
   112	    tss_tol: int = 20,
   113	    min_tss_reads: int = 3,
   114	    tss_frac: float = 0.4,
   115	) -> Tuple[List[Chain], Dict[int, Chain]]:
   116	    """Assemble maximal chains by unambiguous extension.
   117	
   118	    Returns ``(cons_chains, extended_by_index)`` where ``cons_chains[i]`` is input
   119	    chain ``i`` mapped to consensus junctions, and ``extended_by_index[i]`` is the
   120	    maximal chain that chain ``i`` extends into (following only edges from a node
   121	    that has exactly ONE supported successor / predecessor). Two input reads whose
   122	    (possibly truncated) chains lie on the same maximal path map to the SAME
   123	    extended chain, so downstream grouping by the extended chain merges them.
   124	
   125	    5'-TSS brake: if ``five_prime_pos`` (per-input-chain read-5'-end genomic
   126	    positions) is given, a chain whose 5'-terminal junction is a real TSS
   127	    (:func:`anchored_5p_junctions`) is NOT extended in the 5' direction, so
   128	    genuine short isoforms contained inside a longer transcript survive instead of
   129	    being merged away. Without ``five_prime_pos`` behaviour is byte-identical to
   130	    the un-braked assembly (default-off contract).
   131	    """
   132	    cons = cluster_junctions(chains, tol)
   133	    cons_chains: List[Chain] = [tuple(cons[j] for j in ch) for ch in chains]
   134	    _, edge_counts = build_graph(cons_chains)
   135	
   136	    succ: Dict[Junction, List[Junction]] = defaultdict(list)
   137	    pred: Dict[Junction, List[Junction]] = defaultdict(list)
   138	    for (a, b), n in edge_counts.items():
   139	        if n >= min_edge_reads:
   140	            succ[a].append(b)
   141	            pred[b].append(a)
   142	
   143	    anchored: Set[Junction] = set()
   144	    if five_prime_pos is not None:
   145	        anchored = anchored_5p_junctions(
   146	            cons_chains, five_prime_pos, strand, tss_tol, min_tss_reads, tss_frac
   147	        )
   148	
   149	    def unique_succ(j: Junction):
   150	        s = succ.get(j)
   151	        return s[0] if s and len(s) == 1 else None
   152	
   153	    def unique_pred(j: Junction):
   154	        p = pred.get(j)
   155	        return p[0] if p and len(p) == 1 else None
   156	
   157	    cache: Dict[Chain, Chain] = {}
   158	
   159	    def extend(cc: Chain) -> Chain:
   160	        if cc in cache:
   161	            return cache[cc]
   162	        if not cc:
   163	            cache[cc] = cc
   164	            return cc
   165	        chain = list(cc)
   166	        seen: Set[Junction] = set(chain)
   167	        # 5' terminus of THIS read's chain (fixed; 3'-ward extension won't move it)
   168	        five_term = cc[0] if strand == "+" else cc[-1]
   169	        braked = five_term in anchored
   170	        # right extension is 5'-ward on '-' strand, 3'-ward on '+'
   171	        block_right = braked and strand == "-"
   172	        # left extension is 5'-ward on '+' strand, 3'-ward on '-'
   173	        block_left = braked and strand == "+"
   174	        while not block_right:
   175	            nxt = unique_succ(chain[-1])
   176	            if nxt is None or nxt in seen:
   177	                break
   178	            chain.append(nxt)
   179	            seen.add(nxt)
   180	        while not block_left:
   181	            prv = unique_pred(chain[0])
   182	            if prv is None or prv in seen:
   183	                break
   184	            chain.insert(0, prv)
   185	            seen.add(prv)
   186	        out = tuple(chain)
   187	        cache[cc] = out
   188	        return out
   189	
   190	    extended_by_index: Dict[int, Chain] = {
   191	        i: extend(cc) for i, cc in enumerate(cons_chains)
   192	    }
   193	    return cons_chains, extended_by_index
===== fin/io/io_bam.py =====
     1	"""
     2	BAM/SAM file format parser using pysam
     3	"""
     4	
     5	from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Iterator, Tuple, Any, Generator
     6	from pathlib import Path
     7	import logging
     8	
     9	try:
    10	    import pysam
    11	except ImportError:
    12	    raise ImportError(
    13	        "pysam is required for BAM/SAM support. "
    14	        "Install it with: pip install pysam"
    15	    )
    16	
    17	
    18	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19	
    20	
    21	class BamReader:
    22	    """Reader for BAM/SAM alignment files"""
    23	
    24	    def __init__(self, file_path: str, index: bool = True):
    25	        """
    26	        Initialize BAM/SAM reader
    27	
    28	        Args:
    29	            file_path: Path to the BAM/SAM file
    30	            index: Whether to load the index for random access
    31	        """
    32	        self.file_path = Path(file_path)
    33	        if not self.file_path.exists():
    34	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"BAM/SAM file not found: {file_path}")
    35	
    36	        self._alignment_file = None
    37	        self._index = index
    38	        self._is_open = False
    39	
    40	    def __enter__(self):
    41	        """Context manager entry"""
    42	        self.open()
    43	        return self
    44	
    45	    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
    46	        """Context manager exit"""
    47	        self.close()
    48	
    49	    def open(self):
    50	        """Open the alignment file"""
    51	        try:
    52	            mode = 'rb' if self.file_path.suffix.lower() == '.bam' else 'r'
    53	            self._alignment_file = pysam.AlignmentFile(str(self.file_path), mode, index_filename=str(self.file_path) + '.bai' if self._index else None)
    54	            self._is_open = True
    55	
    56	            logger.info(f"Opened alignment file: {self.file_path}")
    57	        except Exception as e:
    58	            logger.error(f"Failed to open alignment file {self.file_path}: {e}")
    59	            raise
    60	
    61	    def close(self):
    62	        """Close the alignment file"""
    63	        if self._alignment_file is not None:
    64	            self._alignment_file.close()
    65	            self._alignment_file = None
    66	            self._is_open = False
    67	            logger.info(f"Closed alignment file: {self.file_path}")
    68	
    69	    @property
    70	    def is_open(self) -> bool:
    71	        """Check if file is open"""
    72	        return self._is_open
    73	
    74	    @property
    75	    def header(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    76	        """
    77	        Get file header
    78	
    79	        Returns:
    80	            Dictionary containing header information
    81	        """
    82	        if self._alignment_file is None:
    83	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
    84	        return dict(self._alignment_file.header)
    85	
    86	    @property
    87	    def references(self) -> List[Tuple[str, int]]:
    88	        """
    89	        Get list of reference sequences
    90	
    91	        Returns:
    92	            List of tuples (reference_name, reference_length)
    93	        """
    94	        if self._alignment_file is None:
    95	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
    96	
    97	        return [(self._alignment_file.get_reference_name(i), self._alignment_file.get_reference_length(i))
    98	                for i in range(self._alignment_file.nreferences)]
    99	
   100	    def count(self, region: Optional[str] = None, read_callback: str = 'all') -> int:
   101	        """
   102	        Count alignments
   103	
   104	        Args:
   105	            region: Region to count (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   106	            read_callback: Read filter (all, mapped, unmapped)
   107	
   108	        Returns:
   109	            Number of alignments
   110	        """
   111	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   112	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   113	
   114	        try:
   115	            return self._alignment_file.count(region=region, read_callback=read_callback)
   116	        except Exception as e:
   117	            logger.error(f"Failed to count alignments: {e}")
   118	            return 0
   119	
   120	    def fetch(self, reference: Optional[str] = None, start: Optional[int] = None,
   121	              end: Optional[int] = None, region: Optional[str] = None,
   122	              until_eof: bool = False) -> Iterator[pysam.AlignedSegment]:
   123	        """
   124	        Fetch alignments from a region
   125	
   126	        Args:
   127	            reference: Reference name
   128	            start: Start position (0-based)
   129	            end: End position
   130	            region: Region string (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   131	            until_eof: Continue to end of file
   132	
   133	        Returns:
   134	            Iterator over AlignedSegment objects
   135	        """
   136	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   137	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   138	
   139	        try:
   140	            # Pass by keyword: pysam.fetch's 5th positional parameter is `tid`,
   141	            # not `until_eof`. Passing until_eof positionally lands it in the tid
   142	            # slot, and tid=False (==0) overrides `contig`, so every region fetch
   143	            # silently returns reads from reference 0 regardless of `reference`.
   144	            return self._alignment_file.fetch(
   145	                contig=reference, start=start, stop=end,
   146	                region=region, until_eof=until_eof,
   147	            )
   148	        except Exception as e:
   149	            logger.error(f"Failed to fetch alignments: {e}")
   150	            return iter([])
   151	
   152	    def get_read(self, read_id: str) -> Optional[pysam.AlignedSegment]:
   153	        """
   154	        Get a specific read by name (requires indexed BAM)
   155	
   156	        Args:
   157	            read_id: Read name/ID
   158	
   159	        Returns:
   160	            AlignedSegment object or None if not found
   161	        """
   162	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   163	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   164	
   165	        try:
   166	            # BAM files can be queried by read name if indexed properly
   167	            # Note: pysam doesn't directly support fetch by name, so we iterate
   168	            # For large files, this is inefficient - better to use fetch with region
   169	            for read in self._alignment_file.fetch(until_eof=True):
   170	                if read.query_name == read_id:
   171	                    return read
   172	            return None
   173	        except Exception as e:
   174	            logger.error(f"Failed to get read {read_id}: {e}")
   175	            return None
   176	
   177	    def complement(self,seq) -> str:
   178	        
   179	        complement_map = {'A': 'T', 'T': 'A', 'C': 'G', 'G': 'C',
   180	                         'N': 'N', 'a': 't', 't': 'a', 'c': 'g', 'g': 'c', 'n': 'n'}
   181	        try:
   182	            return ''.join(complement_map[base] for base in seq)
   183	        except KeyError as e:
   184	            raise ValueError(f"Invalid DNA base: {e}")
   185	
   186	    def reverse_complement(self,seq) -> str:
   187	        if seq:
   188	            return self.complement(seq)[::-1]
   189	        else:
   190	            return None
   191	
   192	    def get_reference_sequence(self, alignment:pysam.AlignedSegment) -> str:
   193	        if alignment.is_secondary:
   194	            return None
   195	        try:
   196	            forward_ref_seq = alignment.get_reference_sequence().upper()
   197	        except ValueError:
   198	            # MD tag not present in BAM (minimap2 without --MD).
   199	            # Downstream consumers default to "" so we can return None safely.
   200	            return None
   201	        return forward_ref_seq if not alignment.is_reverse else self.reverse_complement(forward_ref_seq)
   202	    
   203	    def alignment_to_dict(self, alignment: pysam.AlignedSegment) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   204	        """
   205	        Convert AlignedSegment to dictionary
   206	
   207	        Args:
   208	            alignment: AlignedSegment object
   209	
   210	        Returns:
   211	            Dictionary representation
   212	        """
   213	        if alignment is None:
   214	            return {}
   215	
   216	        return {
   217	            'query_name': alignment.query_name,
   218	            'flag': alignment.flag,
   219	            'is_reverse': alignment.is_reverse,
   220	            'is_secondary': alignment.is_secondary,
   221	            'is_duplicate': alignment.is_duplicate,
   222	            'is_supplementary': alignment.is_supplementary,
   223	            'cigartuples': alignment.cigartuples,
   224	            'reference_name': alignment.reference_name,
   225	            'reference_id': alignment.reference_id,
   226	            'reference_start': alignment.reference_start,
   227	            'reference_end': alignment.reference_end,
   228	            'query_alignment_start': alignment.query_alignment_start,
   229	            'query_alignment_end': alignment.query_alignment_end,
   230	            'query_alignment_length': alignment.query_alignment_length,
   231	            'mapping_quality': alignment.mapping_quality,
   232	            'template_length': alignment.template_length,
   233	            'reference_sequence': self.get_reference_sequence(alignment),
   234	            'is_forward': alignment.is_forward,
   235	            'is_mapped': not alignment.is_unmapped,
   236	            'query_length': alignment.query_length,
   237	            'query_sequence': alignment.query_sequence,
   238	        }
   239	
   240	    def get_reads_in_region(self, region: str, max_reads: Optional[int] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   241	        """
   242	        Get all reads in a specific region
   243	
   244	        Args:
   245	            region: Region string (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   246	            max_reads: Maximum number of reads to return
   247	
   248	        Returns:
   249	            List of read dictionaries
   250	        """
   251	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   252	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   253	
   254	        reads = []
   255	        try:
   256	            for i, alignment in enumerate(self._alignment_file.fetch(region=region)):
   257	                if max_reads is not None and i >= max_reads:
   258	                    break
   259	                reads.append(self.alignment_to_dict(alignment))
   260	        except Exception as e:
   261	            logger.error(f"Failed to get reads in region {region}: {e}")
   262	
   263	        return reads
   264	
   265	    def iterate_alignments(self) -> Generator[Dict[str, Any], None, None]:
   266	        """
   267	        Generator to iterate through all alignments
   268	
   269	        Yields:
   270	            Alignment dictionary
   271	        """
   272	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   273	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   274	
   275	        try:
   276	            for alignment in self._alignment_file.fetch(until_eof=True):
   277	                yield self.alignment_to_dict(alignment)
   278	        except Exception as e:
   279	            logger.error(f"Failed to iterate alignments: {e}")
   280	
   281	    def get_coverage(self, region: str) -> List[int]:
   282	        """
   283	        Get coverage array for a region
   284	
   285	        Args:
   286	            region: Region string (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   287	
   288	        Returns:
   289	            List of coverage values
   290	        """
   291	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   292	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   293	
   294	        try:
   295	            ref_name, start_end = region.split(':')
   296	            start, end = map(int, start_end.split('-'))
   297	
   298	            coverage = [0] * (end - start)
   299	            for alignment in self._alignment_file.fetch(ref_name, start, end):
   300	                if alignment.is_unmapped or alignment.is_secondary or alignment.is_supplementary:
   301	                    continue
   302	
   303	                # Calculate overlap with region
   304	                read_start = max(alignment.reference_start, start)
   305	                read_end = min(alignment.reference_end, end)
   306	
   307	                if read_start < read_end:
   308	                    for pos in range(read_start - start, read_end - start):
   309	                        if 0 <= pos < len(coverage):
   310	                            coverage[pos] += 1
   311	
   312	            return coverage
   313	
   314	        except Exception as e:
   315	            logger.error(f"Failed to get coverage for {region}: {e}")
   316	            return []
   317	
   318	    def get_mate(self, alignment: pysam.AlignedSegment) -> Optional[pysam.AlignedSegment]:
   319	        """
   320	        Get mate alignment for a paired-end read (requires indexed BAM)
   321	
   322	        Args:
   323	            alignment: AlignedSegment object
   324	
   325	        Returns:
   326	            Mate AlignedSegment or None if not found
   327	        """
   328	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   329	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   330	
   331	        if not alignment.is_proper_pair:
   332	            return None
   333	
   334	        try:
   335	            mate = None
   336	            for read in self._alignment_file.fetch(alignment.next_reference_name,
   337	                                                  alignment.next_reference_start,
   338	                                                  alignment.next_reference_start + 1):
   339	                if (read.query_name == alignment.query_name and
   340	                    ((read.is_read1 and alignment.is_read2) or (read.is_read2 and alignment.is_read1))):
   341	                    mate = read
   342	                    break
   343	            return mate
   344	        except Exception as e:
   345	            logger.error(f"Failed to get mate: {e}")
   346	            return None
   347	
   348	    def get_file_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   349	        """
   350	        Get file statistics
   351	
   352	        Returns:
   353	            Dictionary with file statistics
   354	        """
   355	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   356	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   357	
   358	        stats = {
   359	            'filename': str(self.file_path),
   360	            'file_format': 'BAM' if self.file_path.suffix.lower() == '.bam' else 'SAM',
   361	            'nreferences': self._alignment_file.nreferences,
   362	            'references': self.references,
   363	            'header': dict(self._alignment_file.header),
   364	        }
   365	
   366	        # Count various types of reads
   367	        try:
   368	            stats['total_reads'] = self.count()
   369	            stats['mapped_reads'] = self.count(read_callback='mapped')
   370	            stats['unmapped_reads'] = self.count(read_callback='unmapped')
   371	            stats['mapped_pct'] = (stats['mapped_reads'] / stats['total_reads'] * 100) if stats['total_reads'] > 0 else 0
   372	        except Exception:
   373	            stats['total_reads'] = 0
   374	            stats['mapped_reads'] = 0
   375	            stats['unmapped_reads'] = 0
   376	            stats['mapped_pct'] = 0
   377	
   378	        return stats
   379	
   380	    @staticmethod
   381	    def sort_and_index(bam_path: str, output_path: Optional[str] = None) -> bool:
   382	        """
   383	        Sort and index a BAM file
   384	
   385	        Args:
   386	            bam_path: Path to input BAM file
   387	            output_path: Path to output sorted BAM file (default: input + '.sorted.bam')
   388	
   389	        Returns:
   390	            True if successful, False otherwise
   391	        """
   392	        try:
   393	            input_path = Path(bam_path)
   394	            output = Path(output_path) if output_path else input_path.with_suffix('.sorted.bam')
   395	
   396	            # Sort the BAM file
   397	            pysam.sort('-o', str(output), str(input_path))
   398	
   399	            # Index the sorted BAM file
   400	            pysam.index(str(output))
   401	
   402	            logger.info(f"Successfully sorted and indexed BAM: {output}")
   403	            return True
   404	
   405	        except Exception as e:
   406	            logger.error(f"Failed to sort and index BAM: {e}")
   407	            return False
===== fin/io/io_read_manager.py =====
     1	"""
     2	Read Subset Manager for BAM/GTF/FASTA integration
     3	
     4	This module provides classes for organizing nanopore Direct RNA-seq reads into logical subsets
     5	for efficient processing: transcript-specific reads, fusion candidates, and unannotated reads.
     6	Reads are categorized without creating intermediate files until explicitly requested.
     7	"""
     8	
     9	from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Tuple, Set, Any, Generator
    10	from pathlib import Path
    11	from collections import defaultdict
    12	import pysam
    13	
    14	from .io_bam import BamReader
    15	from .io_gtf import GTFReader
    16	
    17	# Import package logger
    18	from ..utils.log_config import get_package_logger
    19	
    20	logger = get_package_logger(__name__)
    21	
    22	
    23	class ReadSubset:
    24	    """Represents a subset of reads for a transcriptomic interval"""
    25	
    26	    def __init__(self, subset_id: str, chrom: str, start: int, end: int,
    27	                 strand: Optional[str] = None, transcript_ids: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    28	                 read_ids: Optional[Set[str]] = None, is_fusion: bool = False,
    29	                 fusion_breakpoints: Optional[List[Tuple[int, int]]] = None):
    30	        """
    31	        Initialize read subset
    32	
    33	        Args:
    34	            subset_id: Unique subset identifier
    35	            chrom: Chromosome name
    36	            start: Start position (0-based)
    37	            end: End position
    38	            strand: Strand information (+, -, or None)
    39	            transcript_ids: List of transcript IDs this subset represents
    40	            read_ids: Set of read IDs in this subset
    41	            is_fusion: Whether this is a fusion candidate subset
    42	            fusion_breakpoints: List of fusion breakpoint coordinates
    43	        """
    44	        self.subset_id = subset_id
    45	        self.chrom = chrom
    46	        self.start = start
    47	        self.end = end
    48	        self.strand = strand
    49	        self.transcript_ids = transcript_ids or []
    50	        self.read_ids = read_ids or set()
    51	        self.is_fusion = is_fusion
    52	        self.fusion_breakpoints = fusion_breakpoints or []
    53	        self.bam_reader = None  # Will be set by manager
    54	
    55	    @property
    56	    def interval(self) -> Tuple[str, int, int]:
    57	        """Get interval as (chrom, start, end) tuple"""
    58	        return (self.chrom, self.start, self.end)
    59	
    60	    @property
    61	    def num_reads(self) -> int:
    62	        """Get number of reads in this subset"""
    63	        return len(self.read_ids)
    64	
    65	    @property
    66	    def region_string(self) -> str:
    67	        """Get region string for BAM fetching"""
    68	        return f"{self.chrom}:{self.start}-{self.end}"
    69	
    70	    def __str__(self) -> str:
    71	        if self.is_fusion:
    72	            tx_info = f"({len(self.fusion_breakpoints)} breakpoints)"
    73	        else:
    74	            tx_info = f"({len(self.transcript_ids)} transcripts)"
    75	        return (f"ReadSubset({self.subset_id}, {self.region_string}, "
    76	                f"{self.num_reads} reads, {tx_info}")
    77	
    78	
    79	class ReadDataBundle:
    80	    """
    81	    Bundle containing reads and associated data for processing
    82	
    83	    This class holds the actual read data, sequence, and annotation information
    84	    for a subset of reads, ready for downstream analysis.
    85	    """
    86	
    87	    def __init__(self, subset: ReadSubset, reads: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    88	                 reference_sequences: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None):
    89	        """
    90	        Initialize read data bundle
    91	
    92	        Args:
    93	            subset: The ReadSubset this bundle corresponds to
    94	            reads: List of read dictionaries from BamReader
    95	            reference_sequences: Dictionary of reference sequences for the region
    96	        """
    97	        self.subset = subset
    98	        self.reads = reads
    99	        self.reference_sequences = reference_sequences or {}
   100	
   101	    @property
   102	    def subset_id(self) -> str:
   103	        """Get subset ID"""
   104	        return self.subset.subset_id
   105	
   106	    @property
   107	    def num_reads(self) -> int:
   108	        """Get number of reads"""
   109	        return len(self.reads)
   110	
   111	    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   112	        """Convert to dictionary representation"""
   113	        return {
   114	            'subset_id': self.subset_id,
   115	            'interval': self.subset.interval,
   116	            'num_reads': self.num_reads,
   117	            'is_fusion': self.subset.is_fusion,
   118	            'transcript_ids': self.subset.transcript_ids,
   119	            'fusion_breakpoints': self.subset.fusion_breakpoints,
   120	            'reads': self.reads,
   121	        }
   122	
   123	
   124	class ReadSubsetManager:
   125	    """
   126	    Manager for organizing reads into structured subsets
   127	
   128	    This class analyzes a BAM file in the context of GTF annotation to group reads
   129	    by transcriptomic intervals. It provides iteration over subsets and can generate
   130	    actual BAM files for each subset when needed.
   131	
   132	    Features:
   133	    - Groups reads by transcriptomic intervals from GTF
   134	    - Identifies fusion candidate reads (multi-mapping, chimeric)
   135	    - Separates unannotated reads into their own subset
   136	    - Allows lazy generation of subset BAM files
   137	    - Ensures reads are unique to one subset (except fusion candidates)
   138	    """
   139	
   140	    def __init__(self, bam_path: str, gtf_path: str, fasta_path: Optional[str] = None):
   141	        """
   142	        Initialize manager
   143	
   144	        Args:
   145	            bam_path: Path to BAM file
   146	            gtf_path: Path to GTF annotation file
   147	            fasta_path: Optional path to reference FASTA file
   148	        """
   149	        self.bam_path = Path(bam_path)
   150	        self.gtf_path = Path(gtf_path)
   151	        self.fasta_path = Path(fasta_path) if fasta_path else None
   152	
   153	        # Readers
   154	        self.bam_reader = BamReader(str(self.bam_path))
   155	        self.gtf_reader = GTFReader(str(self.gtf_path))
   156	
   157	        # Data structures
   158	        self.subsets = []  # List of all ReadSubset objects
   159	        self.fusion_subset = None  # Special subset for fusion candidates
   160	        self.unannotated_subset = None  # Special subset for unannotated reads
   161	        self.read_id_to_subset = {}  # Map read IDs to subset IDs
   162	
   163	        # Caches
   164	        self._fasta_sequences = {}  # Cache for FASTA sequences
   165	        self._reads_by_id = {}  # Cache for read objects
   166	
   167	        logger.info(f"Initialized ReadSubsetManager with BAM: {bam_path}, "
   168	                   f"GTF: {gtf_path}, FASTA: {fasta_path}")
   169	
   170	    def __enter__(self):
   171	        """Context manager entry"""
   172	        self.open()
   173	        return self
   174	
   175	    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
   176	        """Context manager exit"""
   177	        self.close()
   178	
   179	    def open(self):
   180	        """Open all files"""
   181	        self.bam_reader.open()
   182	        self.gtf_reader.open()
   183	        self.gtf_reader.parse()  # Parse GTF to build transcript structure
   184	
   185	        # Load FASTA if available
   186	        if self.fasta_path and self.fasta_path.exists():
   187	            self._load_fasta()
   188	
   189	    def close(self):
   190	        """Close all files"""
   191	        self.bam_reader.close()
   192	        self.gtf_reader.close()
   193	
   194	    def _load_fasta(self):
   195	        """Load reference sequences from FASTA"""
   196	        try:
   197	            from .io_fasta import FASTAReader
   198	            with FASTAReader(str(self.fasta_path)) as fasta_reader:
   199	                for record in fasta_reader.iterate_records():
   200	                    self._fasta_sequences[record.id] = record.sequence
   201	            logger.info(f"Loaded {len(self._fasta_sequences)} reference sequences")
   202	        except Exception as e:
   203	            logger.warning(f"Failed to load FASTA file: {e}")
   204	
   205	    def analyze_reads(self, max_reads: Optional[int] = None):
   206	        """
   207	        Analyze all reads and categorize them into subsets
   208	
   209	        This method iterates through all reads in the BAM file and groups them
   210	        based on their alignment to annotated transcripts or identifies them
   211	        as fusion candidates/unannotated reads.
   212	
   213	        Args:
   214	            max_reads: Optional limit on number of reads to process
   215	        """
   216	        logger.info("Starting read analysis and categorization...")
   217	
   218	        # Initialize special subsets
   219	        self.fusion_subset = ReadSubset(
   220	            subset_id="FUSION_CANDIDATES",
   221	            chrom="multi",
   222	            start=0,
   223	            end=0,
   224	            is_fusion=True
   225	        )
   226	        self.fusion_subset.bam_reader = self.bam_reader
   227	
   228	        self.unannotated_subset = ReadSubset(
   229	            subset_id="UNANNOTATED",
   230	            chrom="unannotated",
   231	            start=0,
   232	            end=0
   233	        )
   234	        self.unannotated_subset.bam_reader = self.bam_reader
   235	
   236	        read_count = 0
   237	        # First pass: collect all read alignments and their intervals
   238	        read_intervals = defaultdict(list)  # read_id -> list of intervals
   239	
   240	        logger.info("First pass: collecting read intervals from BAM...")
   241	        for alignment in self.bam_reader._alignment_file.fetch(until_eof=True):
   242	            if max_reads is not None and read_count >= max_reads:
   243	                break
   244	
   245	            read_dict = self.bam_reader.alignment_to_dict(alignment)
   246	            read_id = read_dict['query_name']
   247	
   248	            # Store read in cache
   249	            self._reads_by_id[read_id] = alignment
   250	
   251	            chrom = read_dict.get('reference_name')
   252	            start = read_dict.get('reference_start', 0)
   253	            end = read_dict.get('reference_end', 0)
   254	
   255	            if chrom and start and end:
   256	                read_intervals[read_id].append((chrom, start, end))
   257	
   258	            read_count += 1
   259	            if read_count % 10000 == 0:
   260	                logger.info(f"Processed {read_count} reads in first pass...")
   261	
   262	        # Build transcript subsets based on BOTH GTF annotation and read coverage
   263	        self._build_transcript_subsets_with_reads(read_intervals)
   264	
   265	        # Second pass: categorize reads
   266	        logger.info("Second pass: categorizing reads...")
   267	        categorized_count = 0
   268	        for alignment in self.bam_reader._alignment_file.fetch(until_eof=True):
   269	            if max_reads is not None and categorized_count >= max_reads:
   270	                break
   271	
   272	            read_dict = self.bam_reader.alignment_to_dict(alignment)
   273	            read_id = read_dict['query_name']
   274	
   275	            # Skip if read_id not in initial pass (shouldn't happen)
   276	            if read_id not in read_intervals:
   277	                continue
   278	
   279	            # Check for fusion candidates first
   280	            if self._is_fusion_candidate(alignment, read_dict):
   281	                self.fusion_subset.read_ids.add(read_id)
   282	                self.read_id_to_subset[read_id] = self.fusion_subset.subset_id
   283	                categorized_count += 1
   284	                continue
   285	
   286	            # Try to assign to transcript-based subset
   287	            assigned = self._assign_to_transcript_subset(alignment, read_dict)
   288	
   289	            if not assigned:
   290	                # Assign to unannotated subset
   291	                self.unannotated_subset.read_ids.add(read_id)
   292	                self.read_id_to_subset[read_id] = self.unannotated_subset.subset_id
   293	
   294	            categorized_count += 1
   295	
   296	            if categorized_count % 10000 == 0:
   297	                logger.info(f"Categorized {categorized_count} reads...")
   298	
   299	        # Filter out empty subsets and add special subsets
   300	        self.subsets = [s for s in self.subsets if s.num_reads > 0]
   301	        self.subsets.append(self.fusion_subset)
   302	        self.subsets.append(self.unannotated_subset)
   303	
   304	        logger.info(f"Completed analysis: {len(self.subsets)} subsets, "
   305	                   f"{categorized_count} total reads categorized")
   306	
   307	        # Sort subsets by chromosome and position
   308	        self.subsets.sort(key=lambda s: (s.chrom, s.start))
   309	
   310	    def _build_transcript_subsets_with_reads(self, read_intervals: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, int, int]]]):
   311	        """
   312	        Build transcript-based subsets considering both GTF annotation and BAM read coverage
   313	
   314	        This ensures subsets are created where there are actually reads aligning, not just where
   315	        there are annotated transcripts.
   316	
   317	        Args:
   318	            read_intervals: Dictionary mapping read_id to list of (chrom, start, end) intervals
   319	        """
   320	        logger.info("Building transcript subsets based on annotation and read coverage...")
   321	
   322	        # Group reads by chromosome and position (clustering)
   323	        chrom_clusters = defaultdict(list)
   324	
   325	        for read_id, intervals in read_intervals.items():
   326	            for chrom, start, end in intervals:
   327	                chrom_clusters[chrom].append((start, end, read_id))
   328	
   329	        # Process each chromosome separately
   330	        subset_counter = 0
   331	        for chrom in sorted(chrom_clusters.keys()):
   332	            if chrom == 'unmapped':
   333	                continue
   334	
   335	            # Sort intervals by start position
   336	            intervals = sorted(chrom_clusters[chrom], key=lambda x: x[0])
   337	
   338	            # Merge overlapping intervals to create clusters
   339	            # Using hierarchical clustering approach
   340	            clusters = []
   341	            current_cluster = []
   342	
   343	            for start, end, read_id in intervals:
   344	                if not current_cluster:
   345	                    current_cluster = [(start, end, read_id)]
   346	                else:
   347	                    # Check if this interval overlaps or is close to current cluster
   348	                    cluster_start = min(s for s, e, r in current_cluster)
   349	                    cluster_end = max(e for s, e, r in current_cluster)
   350	
   351	                    # Merge if there's significant overlap or they're close (< 1000bp)
   352	                    if start <= cluster_end + 1000:
   353	                        current_cluster.append((start, end, read_id))
   354	                    else:
   355	                        # Save current cluster and start new one
   356	                        clusters.append(current_cluster)
   357	                        current_cluster = [(start, end, read_id)]
   358	
   359	            if current_cluster:
   360	                clusters.append(current_cluster)
   361	
   362	            # For each cluster, check if it overlaps with transcripts
   363	            logger.info(f"Processing {len(clusters)} clusters on {chrom}...")
   364	
   365	            for cluster in clusters:
   366	                cluster_start = min(s for s, e, r in cluster)
   367	                cluster_end = max(e for s, e, r in cluster)
   368	
   369	                # Find transcripts that overlap this cluster
   370	                overlapping_transcripts = []
   371	                for transcript in self.gtf_reader.iterate_transcripts():
   372	                    if transcript.chrom == chrom and \
   373	                       transcript.start < cluster_end and transcript.end > cluster_start:
   374	                        overlapping_transcripts.append(transcript)
   375	
   376	                if overlapping_transcripts:
   377	                    # This cluster has annotation - create transcript subset
   378	                    merged_start = min(cluster_start, min(t.start for t in overlapping_transcripts))
   379	                    merged_end = max(cluster_end, max(t.end for t in overlapping_transcripts))
   380	
   381	                    # Determine strand (use majority strand from transcripts)
   382	                    strands = [t.strand for t in overlapping_transcripts if t.strand]
   383	                    strand = max(set(strands), key=strands.count) if strands else None
   384	
   385	                    subset_id = f"TX_{subset_counter:06d}_{chrom}_{cluster_start}_{cluster_end}"
   386	                    subset = ReadSubset(
   387	                        subset_id=subset_id,
   388	                        chrom=chrom,
   389	                        start=merged_start,
   390	                        end=merged_end,
   391	                        strand=strand,
   392	                        transcript_ids=[t.transcript_id for t in overlapping_transcripts]
   393	                    )
   394	                    subset.bam_reader = self.bam_reader
   395	                    self.subsets.append(subset)
   396	                    subset_counter += 1
   397	
   398	                # Note: clusters without transcript overlap will be handled as unannotated
   399	
   400	        logger.info(f"Created {subset_counter} transcript-based subsets")
   401	
   402	    def _build_transcript_subsets(self):
   403	        """Build transcript-based subsets from GTF"""
   404	        subset_counter = 0
   405	
   406	        for transcript in self.gtf_reader.iterate_transcripts():
   407	            # Group transcripts that overlap heavily into the same subset
   408	            # to avoid having too many tiny subsets
   409	            merged = False
   410	            for subset in self.subsets:
   411	                if self._should_merge_transcripts(transcript, subset):
   412	                    subset.transcript_ids.append(transcript.transcript_id)
   413	                    # Update interval to cover both
   414	                    subset.start = min(subset.start, transcript.start)
   415	                    subset.end = max(subset.end, transcript.end)
   416	                    merged = True
   417	                    break
   418	
   419	            if not merged:
   420	                subset_id = f"TX_{subset_counter:06d}_{transcript.chrom}_{transcript.start}_{transcript.end}"
   421	                subset = ReadSubset(
   422	                    subset_id=subset_id,
   423	                    chrom=transcript.chrom,
   424	                    start=transcript.start,
   425	                    end=transcript.end,
   426	                    strand=transcript.strand,
   427	                    transcript_ids=[transcript.transcript_id]
   428	                )
   429	                subset.bam_reader = self.bam_reader
   430	                self.subsets.append(subset)
   431	                subset_counter += 1
   432	
   433	    def _should_merge_transcripts(self, transcript: Any, subset: ReadSubset) -> bool:
   434	        """
   435	        Determine if a transcript should be merged into an existing subset
   436	
   437	        Args:
   438	            transcript: GTF transcript
   439	            subset: Existing subset
   440	
   441	        Returns:
   442	            True if should merge
   443	        """
   444	        if transcript.chrom != subset.chrom:
   445	            return False
   446	
   447	        # Don't merge across strands
   448	        if subset.strand and transcript.strand and subset.strand != transcript.strand:
   449	            return False
   450	
   451	        # Check if overlap is significant (e.g., > 50% of transcript)
   452	        overlap_start = max(subset.start, transcript.start)
   453	        overlap_end = min(subset.end, transcript.end)
   454	
   455	        if overlap_start < overlap_end:
   456	            transcript_length = transcript.end - transcript.start
   457	            overlap_length = overlap_end - overlap_start
   458	
   459	            # Merge if overlap > 50% or gap < 5kb
   460	            gap = max(0, subset.start - transcript.end, transcript.start - subset.end)
   461	            return (overlap_length > transcript_length * 0.5 or
   462	                    (gap < 5000 and overlap_length > 0))
   463	
   464	        return False
   465	
   466	    def _is_fusion_candidate(self, alignment: pysam.AlignedSegment, read_dict: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   467	        """
   468	        Check if a read is a fusion candidate based on soft-clip length
   469	
   470	        Fusion reads typically have long soft-clipped sequences at their ends that didn't
   471	        align to the reference, indicating they span a fusion junction.
   472	
   473	        Args:
   474	            alignment: Pysam alignment object
   475	            read_dict: Dictionary representation of the read
   476	
   477	        Returns:
   478	            True if fusion candidate
   479	        """
   480	        # Default threshold for soft-clip length (can be made configurable)
   481	        SOFT_CLIP_THRESHOLD = 50  # bases
   482	
   483	        if not read_dict.get('cigartuples'):
   484	            return False
   485	
   486	        # Check for long soft-clips at read ends
   487	        cigartuples = read_dict['cigartuples']
   488	
   489	        # Soft-clip operations: 4 (soft clipping)
   490	        # Check first and last CIGAR operations for soft-clips
   491	        first_op, first_len = cigartuples[0]
   492	        last_op, last_len = cigartuples[-1]
   493	
   494	        # Check if soft-clips exceed threshold at either end
   495	        if first_op == 4 and first_len >= SOFT_CLIP_THRESHOLD:
   496	            return True
   497	        if last_op == 4 and last_len >= SOFT_CLIP_THRESHOLD:
   498	            return True
   499	
   500	        return False
   501	
   502	    def _assign_to_transcript_subset(self, alignment: pysam.AlignedSegment,
   503	                                     read_dict: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   504	        """
   505	        Assign read to appropriate transcript-based subset
   506	
   507	        Args:
   508	            alignment: Pysam alignment object
   509	            read_dict: Dictionary representation of the read
   510	
   511	        Returns:
   512	            True if assigned, False if unannotated
   513	        """
   514	        chrom = read_dict.get('reference_name')
   515	        if not chrom:
   516	            return False
   517	
   518	        start = read_dict.get('reference_start', 0)
   519	        end = read_dict.get('reference_end', 0)
   520	
   521	        if not start or not end:
   522	            return False
   523	
   524	        # Find overlapping transcript subsets
   525	        overlapping_subsets = []
   526	        for subset in self.subsets:
   527	            if subset.chrom == chrom and subset.start < end and subset.end > start:
   528	                # Calculate overlap
   529	                overlap_start = max(subset.start, start)
   530	                overlap_end = min(subset.end, end)
   531	                overlap_length = overlap_end - overlap_start
   532	
   533	                if overlap_length > 0:
   534	                    overlapping_subsets.append((subset, overlap_length))
   535	
   536	        if not overlapping_subsets:
   537	            return False
   538	
   539	        # Assign to subset with maximum overlap
   540	        best_subset, _ = max(overlapping_subsets, key=lambda x: x[1])
   541	        best_subset.read_ids.add(read_dict['query_name'])
   542	        self.read_id_to_subset[read_dict['query_name']] = best_subset.subset_id
   543	
   544	        return True
   545	
   546	    def iterate_subsets(self) -> Generator[ReadSubset, None, None]:
   547	        """
   548	        Iterator over all read subsets
   549	
   550	        Yields:
   551	            ReadSubset objects
   552	        """
   553	        yield from self.subsets
   554	
   555	    def get_subset(self, subset_id: str) -> Optional[ReadSubset]:
   556	        """
   557	        Get a specific subset by ID
   558	
   559	        Args:
   560	            subset_id: Subset ID
   561	
   562	        Returns:
   563	            ReadSubset object or None
   564	        """
   565	        for subset in self.subsets:
   566	            if subset.subset_id == subset_id:
   567	                return subset
   568	        return None
   569	
   570	    def get_data_bundle(self, subset: ReadSubset) -> ReadDataBundle:
   571	        """
   572	        Get full data bundle with reads and sequences for a subset
   573	
   574	        Args:
   575	            subset: ReadSubset object
   576	
   577	        Returns:
   578	            ReadDataBundle object
   579	        """
   580	        # Fetch reads for this subset
   581	        reads = []
   582	        for read_id in subset.read_ids:
   583	            if read_id in self._reads_by_id:
   584	                read_dict = self.bam_reader.alignment_to_dict(self._reads_by_id[read_id])
   585	                reads.append(read_dict)
   586	
   587	        # Get reference sequences if FASTA is available
   588	        reference_sequences = {}
   589	        if self.fasta_path and subset.chrom in self._fasta_sequences:
   590	            seq_start = max(0, subset.start - 1000)  # Add some flanking sequence
   591	            seq_end = min(len(self._fasta_sequences[subset.chrom]), subset.end + 1000)
   592	            reference_sequences[subset.chrom] = self._fasta_sequences[subset.chrom][seq_start:seq_end]
   593	
   594	        return ReadDataBundle(subset, reads, reference_sequences)
   595	
   596	    def write_subset_bam(self, subset: ReadSubset, output_path: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
   597	        """
   598	        Write reads from a subset to a new BAM file
   599	
   600	        Args:
   601	            subset: ReadSubset object
   602	            output_path: Output BAM file path (auto-generated if None)
   603	
   604	        Returns:
   605	            Path to created BAM file
   606	        """
   607	        if not output_path:
   608	            output_path = f"{subset.subset_id}.bam"
   609	
   610	        logger.info(f"Writing {subset.num_reads} reads to {output_path}")
   611	
   612	        try:
   613	            # Create output file with same header
   614	            with pysam.AlignmentFile(str(self.bam_path), 'rb') as input_bam:
   615	                with pysam.AlignmentFile(str(output_path), 'wb', template=input_bam) as output_bam:
   616	                    for read_id in subset.read_ids:
   617	                        if read_id in self._reads_by_id:
   618	                            output_bam.write(self._reads_by_id[read_id])
   619	
   620	            # Index the output BAM
   621	            pysam.index(str(output_path))
   622	
   623	            logger.info(f"Successfully created BAM file: {output_path}")
   624	            return str(output_path)
   625	
   626	        except Exception as e:
   627	            logger.error(f"Failed to write subset BAM: {e}")
   628	            raise
   629	
   630	    def get_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   631	        """
   632	        Get summary statistics
   633	
   634	        Returns:
   635	            Dictionary with summary statistics
   636	        """
   637	        return {
   638	            'num_subsets': len(self.subsets),
   639	            'num_fusion_reads': self.fusion_subset.num_reads if self.fusion_subset else 0,
   640	            'num_unannotated_reads': self.unannotated_subset.num_reads if self.unannotated_subset else 0,
   641	            'subset_sizes': [s.num_reads for s in self.subsets],
   642	            'subsets': [{
   643	                'id': s.subset_id,
   644	                'chrom': s.chrom,
   645	                'start': s.start,
   646	                'end': s.end,
   647	                'num_reads': s.num_reads,
   648	                'num_transcripts': len(s.transcript_ids),
   649	                'is_fusion': s.is_fusion
   650	            } for s in self.subsets]
   651	        }
   652	
   653	    def clear_read_cache(self):
   654	        """Clear the read cache to free memory"""
   655	        self._reads_by_id.clear()
   656	
   657	
   658	def create_subset_manager(bam_path: str, gtf_path: str, fasta_path: Optional[str] = None,
   659	                          max_reads: Optional[int] = None) -> ReadSubsetManager:
   660	    """
   661	    Convenience function to create and initialize a ReadSubsetManager
   662	
   663	    Args:
   664	        bam_path: Path to BAM file
   665	        gtf_path: Path to GTF annotation file
   666	        fasta_path: Optional path to FASTA file
   667	        max_reads: Optional limit on number of reads to process
   668	
   669	    Returns:
   670	        Initialized ReadSubsetManager
   671	    """
   672	    manager = ReadSubsetManager(bam_path, gtf_path, fasta_path)
   673	    with manager:
   674	        manager.analyze_reads(max_reads=max_reads)
   675	    return manager
