   332	def discover_candidates(
   333	    interval: GenomicInterval,
   334	    bam_path: str,
   335	    gtf_reader,
   336	    genome_fasta: str,
   337	    threshold: int = 24,
   338	    min_novel_reads: int = 1,
   339	    canonical_search_bp: int = 0,
   340	    max_chains_per_read: int = 16,
   341	    canonical_motifs: tuple = ("GT-AG", "GC-AG", "AT-AC"),
   342	    denovo_wobble_tol: int = 0,
   343	    denovo_wobble_shadow_ratio: float = 1.0,
   344	    denovo_graph: bool = False,
   345	    denovo_graph_tol: int = 6,
   346	    denovo_graph_min_edge_reads: int = 2,
   347	    denovo_graph_tss_brake: bool = True,
   348	    denovo_graph_tss_tol: int = 20,
   349	    denovo_graph_tss_min_reads: int = 3,
   350	    denovo_graph_tss_frac: float = 0.4,
   351	    chain_cluster: bool = False,
   352	    chain_cluster_wobble_bp: int = 6,
   353	    chain_cluster_cassette_max_exon_bp: int = 70,
   354	    chain_cluster_fold_monoexon: bool = False,
   355	    chain_cluster_fold_span_guard: bool = False,
   356	) -> CandidateSet:
   357	    """Discover transcript candidates for a genomic interval.
   358	
   359	    Combines GTF-annotated transcripts with novel isoforms discovered
   360	    from read alignments using intron chain + 3' end clustering.
   361	
   362	    Args:
   363	        interval: Genomic interval to process.
   364	        bam_path: Path to BAM file.
   365	        gtf_reader: Opened GTFReader instance (already parsed).
   366	        genome_fasta: Full chromosome sequence string for the interval's chromosome.
   367	        threshold: 3' end clustering distance in bp.
   368	        canonical_search_bp: If > 0, for every read scan ±N bp around each
   369	            junction's CIGAR position for canonical GT-AG (donor/acceptor)
   370	            motifs and emit the resulting chain alternatives in addition to
   371	            the original chain. 0 disables.
   372	        max_chains_per_read: Hard cap on the per-read alternative chain count
   373	            during canonical search. Reads whose Cartesian-product expansion
   374	            exceeds the cap fall back to their original chain.
   375	
   376	    Returns:
   377	        CandidateSet with discovered candidates.
   378	    """
   379	    from fin.io.io_bam import BamReader
   380	
   381	    # 1. Fetch reads for interval, skip fusion reads
   382	    all_read_ids: Set[str] = set()
   383	    non_fusion_reads = []
   384	    read_sequences: dict = {}
   385	
   386	    with BamReader(bam_path) as bam:
   387	        for alignment in bam.fetch(
   388	            reference=interval.chrom, start=interval.start, end=interval.end
   389	        ):
   390	            rd = bam.alignment_to_dict(alignment)
   391	            if not rd or not rd.get("is_mapped"):
   392	                continue
   393	            # Honor strand-separated intervals. bam.fetch returns reads on BOTH
   394	            # strands overlapping the region, but each read belongs to its own
   395	            # mapped strand's interval. Without this guard an antisense interval
   396	            # that spatially contains a sense interval swallows the sense reads,
   397	            # corrupting strand assignment (a candidate emitted on the wrong
   398	            # strand can never match) and read-to-candidate quantification.
   399	            if interval.strand is not None:
   400	                read_strand = "+" if rd.get("is_forward") else "-"
   401	                if read_strand != interval.strand:
   402	                    continue
   403	            read_id = rd.get("query_name")
   404	            if read_id:
   405	                all_read_ids.add(read_id)
   406	                # Capture primary-alignment sequence only — scope is this
   407	                # interval's fetch, satisfying the per-interval read-set
   408	                # constraint. Used by R1 mappy-argmax bypass.
   409	                if not rd.get("is_secondary") and not rd.get("is_supplementary"):
   410	                    seq = rd.get("query_sequence")
   411	                    if seq:
   412	                        read_sequences.setdefault(read_id, seq)
   413	            if is_fusion_read(rd):
   414	                continue
   415	            non_fusion_reads.append(rd)
   416	
   417	    if not non_fusion_reads:
   418	        return CandidateSet(
   419	            interval=interval,
   420	            candidates=[],
   421	            read_ids=all_read_ids,
   422	            read_sequences=read_sequences,
   423	        )
   424	
   425	    # Determine strand from reads
   426	    strand = interval.strand or "+"
   427	
   428	    # NEW generation-side clustering (production default). Group reads by intron chain
   429	    # (3' ignored), collapse EXACT sub-chains, cluster wobble/cassette/containment
   430	    # keeping members; NO canonical-search shadow generation. Bypasses the legacy
   431	    # 3'+exact-chain grouping and canonical expansion entirely.
   432	    if chain_cluster:
   433	        return _chain_cluster_candidates(
   434	            interval, non_fusion_reads, all_read_ids, read_sequences,
   435	            gtf_reader, genome_fasta, strand, min_novel_reads,
   436	            wobble_bp=chain_cluster_wobble_bp,
   437	            cassette_max_exon_bp=chain_cluster_cassette_max_exon_bp,
   438	            fold_monoexon_contained=chain_cluster_fold_monoexon,
   439	            fold_span_guard=chain_cluster_fold_span_guard,
   440	        )
   441	
   442	    # 2. Group reads by 3' + intron chain → novel candidate groups.
   443	    # Optional pre-processing:
   444	    #   - canonical_search_bp>0: for each junction, scan the genome ±N bp for
   445	    #     canonical GT-AG motifs and emit the resulting chain alternatives
   446	    #     alongside the original (recovering ambiguous-GT-AG strict misses
   447	    #     where multiple valid splice sites exist within a few bp).
   448	    multi_chain_override = None
   449	
   450	    if canonical_search_bp > 0:
   451	        # Pre-extract CIGAR-derived chains once.
   452	        read_chains = []
   453	        for rd in non_fusion_reads:
   454	            cigar = rd.get("cigartuples")
   455	            ref_start = rd.get("reference_start")
   456	            if cigar is None or ref_start is None:
   457	                continue
   458	            read_chains.append((rd, extract_intron_chain(cigar, ref_start)))
   459	
   460	        multi_chain_override = expand_canonical_chain_alternatives_v2(
   461	            read_chains, genome_fasta, strand,
   462	            motif_set=parse_motifs(canonical_motifs),
   463	            search_bp=canonical_search_bp,
   464	            max_chains_per_read=max_chains_per_read,
   465	        )
   466	
   467	    if denovo_graph:
   468	        # Intron-graph assembly: group reads by their EXTENDED (assembled) chain
   469	        # so truncated partials become one full-length candidate. Takes precedence
   470	        # over canonical-search expansion.
   471	        groups = _graph_groups(
   472	            non_fusion_reads, strand, denovo_graph_tol, denovo_graph_min_edge_reads,
   473	            tss_brake=denovo_graph_tss_brake, tss_tol=denovo_graph_tss_tol,
   474	            tss_min_reads=denovo_graph_tss_min_reads, tss_frac=denovo_graph_tss_frac,
   475	        )
   476	    else:
   477	        groups = group_reads_by_three_prime_and_intron_chain(
   478	            non_fusion_reads, strand, threshold=threshold,
   479	            multi_chain_override=multi_chain_override,
   480	        )
   481	
   482	    # 3. Get GTF transcripts in region
   483	    gtf_transcripts = []
   484	    if gtf_reader is not None:
   485	        gtf_transcripts = gtf_reader.get_transcripts_in_region(
   486	            interval.chrom, interval.start, interval.end
   487	        )
   488	
   489	    # 4. Build GTF candidates with intron chains
   490	    gtf_candidates = []
   491	    for tx in gtf_transcripts:
   492	        # Strand-separated interval: skip opposite-strand annotation (mirror of the
   493	        # chain-cluster path). No effect when the interval is unstranded / no GTF.
   494	        if interval.strand is not None and tx.strand != interval.strand:
   495	            continue
   496	        chain = gtf_transcript_to_intron_chain(tx)
   497	        tx.sort_features()
   498	        # Get 3' position from GTF
   499	        if tx.strand == "+":
   500	            three_prime = tx.end
   501	        else:
   502	            three_prime = tx.start
   503	
   504	        seq = tx.get_spliced_sequence(genome_fasta)
   505	
   506	        gtf_candidates.append(
   507	            TranscriptCandidate(
   508	                candidate_id=tx.transcript_id,
   509	                intron_chain=chain,
   510	                three_prime_pos=three_prime,
   511	                sequence=seq,
   512	                source="gtf",
   513	                supporting_read_ids=set(),
   514	                chrom=interval.chrom,
   515	                strand=tx.strand,
   516	                start=tx.start,
   517	                end=tx.end,
   518	            )
   519	        )
   520	
   521	    # 5. Match novel groups vs GTF: same intron chain + 3' within threshold → merge
   522	    candidates: List[TranscriptCandidate] = list(gtf_candidates)
   523	    matched_group_keys: Set = set()
   524	
   525	    for group_key, reads in groups.items():

   134	
   135	def is_fusion_read(read_dict: Dict) -> bool:
   136	    """
   137	    Check if a read is a fusion candidate based on soft-clip length
   138	
   139	    Args:
   140	        read_dict: Dictionary representation of the read from BamReader
   141	
   142	    Returns:
   143	        True if read is a fusion candidate
   144	    """
   145	    
   146	    if read_dict.get('is_supplementary'):
   147	        return True
   148	    
   149	    SOFT_CLIP_THRESHOLD = 250
   150	    # logger.debug(f'Checking Fusion reads:{read_dict.get("cigartuples")}')
   151	    if not read_dict.get('cigartuples'):
   152	        return False
   153	
   154	    # Check for long soft-clips at read ends
   155	    cigartuples = read_dict['cigartuples']
   156	    if not cigartuples:
   157	        return False
   158	
   159	    # Check first and last CIGAR operations for soft-clips
   160	    first_op, first_len = cigartuples[0]
   161	    last_op, last_len = cigartuples[-1]
   162	
   163	    # Check if soft-clips exceed threshold at either end
   164	    if first_op == 4 and first_len >= SOFT_CLIP_THRESHOLD:
   165	        return True
   166	    if last_op == 4 and last_len >= SOFT_CLIP_THRESHOLD:
   167	        return True
   168	    
   169	    
   170	    # OR I can just use read_dict.get('is_supplementary')
   171	    return False
   172	
   173	
   174	def extract_strand_from_read(read_dict: Dict) -> Optional[str]:
   175	    """
   176	    Extract strand information from a read alignment
   177	
   178	    For some BAM files, strand may not be directly available.
   179	    This function can be extended based on specific needs.
   180	
   181	    Args:
   182	        read_dict: Dictionary representation of the read
   183	
   184	    Returns:
   185	        Strand ('+' or '-') or None if not available
   186	    """
   187	    # Placeholder - can be extended based on specific BAM format
   188	    # Some BAM files store strand in flags or tags
   189	    return '+' if read_dict.get('is_forward') else '-'
   190	
   191	def extract_three_prime_pos(read_dict: Dict) -> Optional[int]:
   192	    """Return the 3' genomic coordinate for a read, or None if unmapped.
   193	
   194	    Forward reads -> reference_end (exclusive); reverse reads -> reference_start.
   195	    pysam returns reference_end=None for unmapped reads; we must propagate
   196	    None instead of TypeError'ing inside int().
   197	    """
   198	    pos = (
   199	        read_dict.get('reference_end')
   200	        if read_dict.get('is_forward')
   201	        else read_dict.get('reference_start')
   202	    )
   203	    if pos is None:
   204	        return None
   205	    return int(pos)
   206	
   207	def generate_intervals_from_reads(bam_path: str, max_reads: Optional[int] = None) -> Tuple[List[Dict], Set[str], List[Dict]]:
   208	    """
   209	    Generate intervals from BAM read alignments
   210	
   211	    Args:
   212	        bam_path: Path to BAM file
   213	        max_reads: Optional limit on number of reads to process
   214	
   215	    Returns:
   216	        Tuple of (read_alignments, fusion_read_ids, fusion_reads)
   217	    """
   218	    logger.info("Processing BAM reads for interval generation...")
   219	
   220	    fusion_read_ids = set()
   221	    fusion_reads = []
   222	    read_alignments = []
   223	
   224	    with BamReader(bam_path) as bam_reader:
   225	        bam_reader.open()
   226	
   227	        read_count = 0
   228	        skipped_unmapped = 0
   229	        for alignment in bam_reader._alignment_file.fetch(until_eof=True):
   230	            if max_reads is not None and read_count >= max_reads:
   231	                break
   232	
   233	            # Skip unmapped / secondary early. Unmapped reads would otherwise hit
   234	            # get_reference_sequence() -> ValueError on every iteration; in BAMs
   235	            # dominated by unmapped reads this is the hot path. Supplementary
   235	            # dominated by unmapped reads this is the hot path. Supplementary
   236	            # alignments are kept because is_fusion_read() depends on them.
   237	            if alignment.is_unmapped or alignment.is_secondary:
   238	                skipped_unmapped += 1
   239	                continue
   240	
   241	            read_dict = bam_reader.alignment_to_dict(alignment)
   242	
   243	            # Check for fusion reads
   244	            # logger.debug(f'Checking if {read_dict["query_name"]} is a fusion-read')
   245	            if is_fusion_read(read_dict):
   246	                logger.debug(f'{read_dict["query_name"]} is a fusion-read, skipping')
   247	                fusion_read_ids.add(read_dict['query_name'])
   248	                fusion_reads.append(read_dict)
   249	                read_count += 1
   250	                if read_count % 10000 == 0:
   251	                    logger.info(f"Processed {read_count} reads...")
   252	                continue
   253	
   254	            # Keep the read alignment info
   255	            read_alignments.append(read_dict)
   256	            read_count += 1
   257	
   258	            if read_count % 10000 == 0:
   259	                logger.info(f"Processed {read_count} reads...")
   260	
   261	    logger.info(
   262	        f"Collected {len(read_alignments)} read alignments, "
   263	        f"{len(fusion_read_ids)} fusion reads ({len(fusion_reads)} fusion read dicts retained), "
   264	        f"skipped {skipped_unmapped} unmapped/secondary"
   265	    )
   266	    return read_alignments, fusion_read_ids, fusion_reads
   267	
   268	
   269	def cluster_intervals(read_alignments: List[Dict], max_gap: int = 0) -> List[GenomicInterval]:
   270	    """
   271	    Cluster overlapping or nearby intervals by chromosome and strand
   272	
   273	    Args:
   274	        read_alignments: List of read alignment dictionaries
   275	        max_gap: Maximum gap between intervals to merge (default: 0bp)
   276	
   277	    Returns:
   278	        List of clustered GenomicInterval objects (strand-separated)
   279	    """
   280	    if not read_alignments:
   281	        return []
   282	
   283	    logger.info(f"Clustering {len(read_alignments)} read alignments with max_gap={max_gap}...")
   284	
   285	    # Group by chromosome and strand
   286	    chrom_strand_intervals = defaultdict(list)
   287	    for read_dict in read_alignments:
   288	        chrom = read_dict.get('reference_name')
   289	        strand = extract_strand_from_read(read_dict) 
   290	        start = read_dict.get('reference_start')
   291	        end = read_dict.get('reference_end')
   292	        read_id = read_dict.get('query_name')
   293	        three_prime_pos = extract_three_prime_pos(read_dict)
   294	
   295	        if chrom and start is not None and end is not None:
   296	            key = (chrom, strand)  # Separate by strand
   297	            chrom_strand_intervals[key].append((start, end, read_id, three_prime_pos))
   298	
   299	    clustered = []
   300	    interval_counter = 0
   301	
   302	    for (chrom, strand), intervals in sorted(chrom_strand_intervals.items()):
   303	        if not intervals:
   304	            continue
   305	
   306	        # Count reads per position for proper read counting
   307	        # position_counts = defaultdict(set)  # position -> set of read_ids
   308	        # for start, end, read_id in intervals:
   309	        #     # Mark all positions in the interval as covered by this read
   310	        #     for pos in range(start, end):
   311	        #         position_counts[pos].add(read_id)
   312	
   313	        # Sort intervals by start position
   314	        intervals_sorted = sorted(intervals, key=lambda x: x[0])
   315	
   316	        current_cluster = None
   317	        current_reads = list()
   318	        current_three_prime_pos = list()
   319	        for start, end, read_id, three_prime_pos in intervals_sorted:
   320	            if current_cluster is None:
   321	                # Start new cluster
   322	                current_cluster = (start, end)
   323	                current_reads = [read_id]
   324	                current_three_prime_pos = [three_prime_pos]
   325	            elif start <= current_cluster[1] + max_gap:
   326	                # Merge with current cluster
   327	                current_cluster = (
   328	                    current_cluster[0],
   329	                    max(current_cluster[1], end)
   330	                )
   331	                current_reads.append(read_id)
   332	                current_three_prime_pos.append(three_prime_pos)
   333	            else:
   334	                # Save current cluster and start new one
   335	                clustered.append(GenomicInterval(
   336	                    chrom=chrom,
   337	                    start=current_cluster[0],
   338	                    end=current_cluster[1],
   339	                    strand=strand,
   340	                    read_count=len(current_reads),
   341	                    interval_id=f"interval_{interval_counter:06d}",
   342	                    attrs=current_reads,
   343	                    three_prime_pos=current_three_prime_pos
   344	                    
   345	                ))
   346	                interval_counter += 1
   347	                current_cluster = (start, end)
   348	                current_reads = [read_id]
   349	                current_three_prime_pos = [three_prime_pos]
   350	                
   351	
   352	        # Save the last cluster
   353	        if current_cluster:
   354	            clustered.append(GenomicInterval(
   355	                chrom=chrom,
   356	                start=current_cluster[0],
   357	                end=current_cluster[1],
   358	                strand=strand,
   359	                read_count=len(current_reads),
   360	                interval_id=f"interval_{interval_counter:06d}",
   361	                attrs=current_reads,
   362	                three_prime_pos=current_three_prime_pos
   363	            ))
   364	            interval_counter += 1
   365	
   366	    logger.info(f"Created {len(clustered)} clustered intervals (strand-separated)")
   367	    return clustered
   368	
   369	
   370	def generate_intervals_from_gtf(gtf_path: str) -> List[GenomicInterval]:
   476	def generate_isolated_intervals(bam_path: str,
   477	                                gtf_path: Optional[str] = None,
   478	                                max_gap: int =0,
   479	                                max_reads: Optional[int] = None, tmp_dir: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   480	    """
   481	    Generate isolated, non-overlapping genomic intervals with strand separation
   482	
   483	    This is the main function that generates intervals by analyzing both
   484	    GTF annotation and BAM read alignments. Fusion reads are identified
   485	    and skipped during interval generation.
   486	
   487	    Args:
   488	        bam_path: Path to BAM file (required)
   489	        gtf_path: Path to GTF file (optional, improves annotation)
   490	        max_gap: Maximum gap between intervals to merge (default: 0)
   491	        max_reads: Optional limit on number of reads to process
   492	
   493	    Returns:
   494	        Dictionary with:
   495	        - 'intervals': List of GenomicInterval objects (strand-separated)
   496	        - 'fusion_read_ids': Set of fusion read IDs
   497	        - 'fusion_reads': List of full read dicts for fusion reads
   498	        - 'num_reads_processed': Number of reads processed
   499	    """
   500	    logger.info("=" * 60)
   501	    logger.info("Starting interval generation")
   502	    logger.info(f"BAM: {bam_path}")
   503	    logger.info(f"GTF: {gtf_path}")
   504	    logger.info(f"Max gap: {max_gap}bp")
   505	    logger.info("=" * 60)
   506	
   507	    # Process BAM reads
   508	    read_alignments, fusion_read_ids, fusion_reads = generate_intervals_from_reads(bam_path, max_reads)
   509	
   510	    # Cluster read alignments
   511	    read_intervals = cluster_intervals(read_alignments, max_gap)
   512	    # logger.debug(read_intervals)
   513	    if tmp_dir:
   514	        Path(tmp_dir).mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   515	        tmp_read_intervals = Path(tmp_dir) / "read_intervals.bed"
   516	        # tmp_read_intervals.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   517	        intervals_to_bed(read_intervals,tmp_read_intervals)
   518	    # Generate intervals from GTF if provided
   519	    gtf_intervals = []
   520	    if gtf_path:
   521	        gtf_intervals = generate_intervals_from_gtf(gtf_path)
   522	    if tmp_dir:
   523	        tmp_gtf_intervals = Path(tmp_dir) / "gtf_intervals.bed"
   524	        # tmp_gtf_intervals.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   525	        intervals_to_bed(gtf_intervals,tmp_gtf_intervals)
   526	    # Merge intervals
   527	    final_intervals = merge_gtf_and_read_intervals(
   528	        gtf_intervals,
   529	        read_intervals,
   530	        max_gap=max_gap
   531	    )
   532	    # logger.debug(final_intervals)
   533	    # Sort intervals by chromosome, strand, position
   534	    strand_order = {'+': 0, '-': 1, None: 2}
   535	    final_intervals.sort(key=lambda x: (x.chrom, strand_order.get(x.strand, 2), x.start))
   536	    if tmp_dir:
   537	        tmp_final_intervals = Path(tmp_dir) / "final_intervals.bed"
   538	        # tmp_final_intervals.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   539	        intervals_to_bed(final_intervals,tmp_final_intervals)
   540	    # Log summary
   541	    logger.info("=" * 60)
   542	    logger.info("Interval generation completed")
   543	    logger.info(f"Total intervals: {len(final_intervals)}")
   544	    logger.info(f"Fusion reads detected: {len(fusion_read_ids)}")
   545	
   546	    # Log intervals by strand
   547	    strand_counts = defaultdict(int)
   548	    for interval in final_intervals:
   549	        strand_counts[interval.strand] += 1
   550	
   551	    
   552	    logger.info("Intervals by strand:")
   553	    logger.debug(strand_counts.items())
   554	    for strand, count in sorted(strand_counts.items()):
   555	        logger.info(f"  {strand if strand else 'unstranded'}: {count}")
   556	
   557	    logger.info("=" * 60)
   558	
   559	    # Log first 5 intervals
   560	    logger.info("First 5 intervals:")
   561	    for i, interval in enumerate(final_intervals[:5], 1):
   562	        logger.info(f"  {i}. {interval.region_string} [strand: {interval.strand}] "
   563	                   f"(reads: {interval.read_count})")
   564	
   565	    if len(final_intervals) > 5:
   566	        logger.info(f"  ... and {len(final_intervals) - 5} more intervals")
   567	
   568	    return {
   569	        'intervals': final_intervals,
   570	        'fusion_read_ids': fusion_read_ids,
   571	        'fusion_reads': fusion_reads,
   572	        'num_reads_processed': len(read_alignments) + len(fusion_read_ids)
   573	    }
   574	
   575	
   576	def extract_reads_for_interval(bam_path: str,
   577	                               interval: GenomicInterval,
   578	                               fusion_read_ids: Optional[Set[str]] = None) -> List[Dict]:
   579	    """
   580	    Extract reads for a specific interval from BAM file
   581	
   582	    Args:
   583	        bam_path: Path to BAM file
   584	        interval: GenomicInterval to extract reads for
   585	        fusion_read_ids: Optional set of fusion read IDs to skip
   586	
   587	    Returns:
   588	        List of read dictionaries
   589	    """
   590	    if fusion_read_ids is None:
   591	        fusion_read_ids = set()
   592	
   593	    reads = []
   594	
   595	    with BamReader(bam_path) as bam_reader:
   596	        bam_reader.open()
   597	
   598	        # Fetch reads from the interval
   599	        for alignment in bam_reader._alignment_file.fetch(
   600	            interval.chrom,
   601	            interval.start,
   602	            interval.end
   603	        ):
   604	            read_dict = bam_reader.alignment_to_dict(alignment)
   605	            read_id = read_dict['query_name']
   606	
   607	            # Skip fusion reads
   608	            if read_id in fusion_read_ids or is_fusion_read(read_dict):
   609	                logger.debug("%s is a fusion-read, skipping", read_id)
   610	                continue
   611	
   612	            # Check if read actually overlaps
   613	            read_start = read_dict.get('reference_start')
   614	            read_end = read_dict.get('reference_end')
   615	
   616	            if (read_start is not None and read_end is not None and
   617	                read_start < interval.end and read_end > interval.start):
   618	                reads.append(read_dict)
   619	
   620	    logger.debug(f"Extracted {len(reads)} reads for interval {interval.region_string}")
   621	    return reads

   624	def extract_annotation_for_interval(gtf_path: str,

     1	"""
     2	BAM/SAM file format parser using pysam
     3	"""
     4	
     5	from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Iterator, Tuple, Any, Generator
     6	from pathlib import Path
     7	import logging
     8	
     9	try:
    10	    import pysam
    11	except ImportError:
    12	    raise ImportError(
    13	        "pysam is required for BAM/SAM support. "
    14	        "Install it with: pip install pysam"
    15	    )
    16	
    17	
    18	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19	
    20	
    21	class BamReader:
    22	    """Reader for BAM/SAM alignment files"""
    23	
    24	    def __init__(self, file_path: str, index: bool = True):
    25	        """
    26	        Initialize BAM/SAM reader
    27	
    28	        Args:
    29	            file_path: Path to the BAM/SAM file
    30	            index: Whether to load the index for random access
    31	        """
    32	        self.file_path = Path(file_path)
    33	        if not self.file_path.exists():
    34	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"BAM/SAM file not found: {file_path}")
    35	
    36	        self._alignment_file = None
    37	        self._index = index
    38	        self._is_open = False
    39	
    40	    def __enter__(self):
    41	        """Context manager entry"""
    42	        self.open()
    43	        return self
    44	
    45	    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
    46	        """Context manager exit"""
    47	        self.close()
    48	
    49	    def open(self):
    50	        """Open the alignment file"""
    51	        try:
    52	            mode = 'rb' if self.file_path.suffix.lower() == '.bam' else 'r'
    53	            self._alignment_file = pysam.AlignmentFile(str(self.file_path), mode, index_filename=str(self.file_path) + '.bai' if self._index else None)
    54	            self._is_open = True
    55	
    56	            logger.info(f"Opened alignment file: {self.file_path}")
    57	        except Exception as e:
    58	            logger.error(f"Failed to open alignment file {self.file_path}: {e}")
    59	            raise
    60	
    61	    def close(self):
    62	        """Close the alignment file"""
    63	        if self._alignment_file is not None:
    64	            self._alignment_file.close()
    65	            self._alignment_file = None
    66	            self._is_open = False
    67	            logger.info(f"Closed alignment file: {self.file_path}")
    68	
    69	    @property
    70	    def is_open(self) -> bool:
    71	        """Check if file is open"""
    72	        return self._is_open
    73	
    74	    @property
    75	    def header(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    76	        """
    77	        Get file header
    78	
    79	        Returns:
    80	            Dictionary containing header information
    81	        """
    82	        if self._alignment_file is None:
    83	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
    84	        return dict(self._alignment_file.header)
    85	
    86	    @property
    87	    def references(self) -> List[Tuple[str, int]]:
    88	        """
    89	        Get list of reference sequences
    90	
    91	        Returns:
    92	            List of tuples (reference_name, reference_length)
    93	        """
    94	        if self._alignment_file is None:
    95	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
    96	
    97	        return [(self._alignment_file.get_reference_name(i), self._alignment_file.get_reference_length(i))
    98	                for i in range(self._alignment_file.nreferences)]
    99	
   100	    def count(self, region: Optional[str] = None, read_callback: str = 'all') -> int:
   101	        """
   102	        Count alignments
   103	
   104	        Args:
   105	            region: Region to count (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   106	            read_callback: Read filter (all, mapped, unmapped)
   107	
   108	        Returns:
   109	            Number of alignments
   110	        """
   111	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   112	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   113	
   114	        try:
   115	            return self._alignment_file.count(region=region, read_callback=read_callback)
   116	        except Exception as e:
   117	            logger.error(f"Failed to count alignments: {e}")
   118	            return 0
   119	
   120	    def fetch(self, reference: Optional[str] = None, start: Optional[int] = None,
   121	              end: Optional[int] = None, region: Optional[str] = None,
   122	              until_eof: bool = False) -> Iterator[pysam.AlignedSegment]:
   123	        """
   124	        Fetch alignments from a region
   125	
   126	        Args:
   127	            reference: Reference name
   128	            start: Start position (0-based)
   129	            end: End position
   130	            region: Region string (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   131	            until_eof: Continue to end of file
   132	
   133	        Returns:
   134	            Iterator over AlignedSegment objects
   135	        """
   136	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   137	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   138	
   139	        try:
   140	            # Pass by keyword: pysam.fetch's 5th positional parameter is `tid`,
   141	            # not `until_eof`. Passing until_eof positionally lands it in the tid
   142	            # slot, and tid=False (==0) overrides `contig`, so every region fetch
   143	            # silently returns reads from reference 0 regardless of `reference`.
   144	            return self._alignment_file.fetch(
   145	                contig=reference, start=start, stop=end,
   146	                region=region, until_eof=until_eof,
   147	            )
   148	        except Exception as e:
   149	            logger.error(f"Failed to fetch alignments: {e}")
   150	            return iter([])
   151	
   152	    def get_read(self, read_id: str) -> Optional[pysam.AlignedSegment]:
   153	        """
   154	        Get a specific read by name (requires indexed BAM)
   155	
   156	        Args:
   157	            read_id: Read name/ID
   158	
   159	        Returns:
   160	            AlignedSegment object or None if not found
   161	        """
   162	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   163	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   164	
   165	        try:
   166	            # BAM files can be queried by read name if indexed properly
   167	            # Note: pysam doesn't directly support fetch by name, so we iterate
   168	            # For large files, this is inefficient - better to use fetch with region
   169	            for read in self._alignment_file.fetch(until_eof=True):
   170	                if read.query_name == read_id:
   171	                    return read
   172	            return None
   173	        except Exception as e:
   174	            logger.error(f"Failed to get read {read_id}: {e}")
   175	            return None
   176	
   177	    def complement(self,seq) -> str:
   178	        
   179	        complement_map = {'A': 'T', 'T': 'A', 'C': 'G', 'G': 'C',
   180	                         'N': 'N', 'a': 't', 't': 'a', 'c': 'g', 'g': 'c', 'n': 'n'}
   181	        try:
   182	            return ''.join(complement_map[base] for base in seq)
   183	        except KeyError as e:
   184	            raise ValueError(f"Invalid DNA base: {e}")
   185	
   186	    def reverse_complement(self,seq) -> str:
   187	        if seq:
   188	            return self.complement(seq)[::-1]
   189	        else:
   190	            return None
   191	
   192	    def get_reference_sequence(self, alignment:pysam.AlignedSegment) -> str:
   193	        if alignment.is_secondary:
   194	            return None
   195	        try:
   196	            forward_ref_seq = alignment.get_reference_sequence().upper()
   197	        except ValueError:
   198	            # MD tag not present in BAM (minimap2 without --MD).
   199	            # Downstream consumers default to "" so we can return None safely.
   200	            return None
   201	        return forward_ref_seq if not alignment.is_reverse else self.reverse_complement(forward_ref_seq)
   202	    
   203	    def alignment_to_dict(self, alignment: pysam.AlignedSegment) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   204	        """
   205	        Convert AlignedSegment to dictionary
   206	
   207	        Args:
   208	            alignment: AlignedSegment object
   209	
   210	        Returns:
   211	            Dictionary representation
   212	        """
   213	        if alignment is None:
   214	            return {}
   215	
   216	        return {
   217	            'query_name': alignment.query_name,
   218	            'flag': alignment.flag,
   219	            'is_reverse': alignment.is_reverse,
   220	            'is_secondary': alignment.is_secondary,
   221	            'is_duplicate': alignment.is_duplicate,
   222	            'is_supplementary': alignment.is_supplementary,
   223	            'cigartuples': alignment.cigartuples,
   224	            'reference_name': alignment.reference_name,
   225	            'reference_id': alignment.reference_id,
   226	            'reference_start': alignment.reference_start,
   227	            'reference_end': alignment.reference_end,
   228	            'query_alignment_start': alignment.query_alignment_start,
   229	            'query_alignment_end': alignment.query_alignment_end,
   230	            'query_alignment_length': alignment.query_alignment_length,
   231	            'mapping_quality': alignment.mapping_quality,
   232	            'template_length': alignment.template_length,
   233	            'reference_sequence': self.get_reference_sequence(alignment),
   234	            'is_forward': alignment.is_forward,
   235	            'is_mapped': not alignment.is_unmapped,
   236	            'query_length': alignment.query_length,
   237	            'query_sequence': alignment.query_sequence,
   238	        }
   239	
   240	    def get_reads_in_region(self, region: str, max_reads: Optional[int] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   241	        """
   242	        Get all reads in a specific region
   243	
   244	        Args:
   245	            region: Region string (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   246	            max_reads: Maximum number of reads to return
   247	
   248	        Returns:
   249	            List of read dictionaries
   250	        """
   251	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   252	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   253	
   254	        reads = []
   255	        try:
   256	            for i, alignment in enumerate(self._alignment_file.fetch(region=region)):
   257	                if max_reads is not None and i >= max_reads:
   258	                    break
   259	                reads.append(self.alignment_to_dict(alignment))
   260	        except Exception as e:

--- fusion + strand ---
   135	def is_fusion_read(read_dict: Dict) -> bool:
   136	    """
   137	    Check if a read is a fusion candidate based on soft-clip length
   138	
   139	    Args:
   140	        read_dict: Dictionary representation of the read from BamReader
   141	
   142	    Returns:
   143	        True if read is a fusion candidate
   144	    """
   145	    
   146	    if read_dict.get('is_supplementary'):
   147	        return True
   148	    
   149	    SOFT_CLIP_THRESHOLD = 250
   150	    # logger.debug(f'Checking Fusion reads:{read_dict.get("cigartuples")}')
   151	    if not read_dict.get('cigartuples'):
   152	        return False
   153	
   154	    # Check for long soft-clips at read ends
   155	    cigartuples = read_dict['cigartuples']
   156	    if not cigartuples:
   157	        return False
   158	
   159	    # Check first and last CIGAR operations for soft-clips
   160	    first_op, first_len = cigartuples[0]
   161	    last_op, last_len = cigartuples[-1]
   162	
   163	    # Check if soft-clips exceed threshold at either end
   164	    if first_op == 4 and first_len >= SOFT_CLIP_THRESHOLD:
   165	        return True
   166	    if last_op == 4 and last_len >= SOFT_CLIP_THRESHOLD:
   167	        return True
   168	    
   169	    
   170	    # OR I can just use read_dict.get('is_supplementary')
   171	    return False
   172	
   173	
   174	def extract_strand_from_read(read_dict: Dict) -> Optional[str]:
   175	    """
   176	    Extract strand information from a read alignment
   177	
   178	    For some BAM files, strand may not be directly available.
   179	    This function can be extended based on specific needs.
   180	
   181	    Args:
   182	        read_dict: Dictionary representation of the read
   183	
   184	    Returns:
   185	        Strand ('+' or '-') or None if not available
   186	    """
   187	    # Placeholder - can be extended based on specific BAM format
   188	    # Some BAM files store strand in flags or tags
   189	    return '+' if read_dict.get('is_forward') else '-'
   190	
   191	def extract_three_prime_pos(read_dict: Dict) -> Optional[int]:
   192	    """Return the 3' genomic coordinate for a read, or None if unmapped.
   193	
   194	    Forward reads -> reference_end (exclusive); reverse reads -> reference_start.
   195	    pysam returns reference_end=None for unmapped reads; we must propagate
   196	    None instead of TypeError'ing inside int().
   197	    """
   198	    pos = (
   199	        read_dict.get('reference_end')
   200	        if read_dict.get('is_forward')
   201	        else read_dict.get('reference_start')
   202	    )
   203	    if pos is None:
   204	        return None
   205	    return int(pos)
   206	
   207	def generate_intervals_from_reads(bam_path: str, max_reads: Optional[int] = None) -> Tuple[List[Dict], Set[str], List[Dict]]:
   208	    """
   209	    Generate intervals from BAM read alignments
   210	
   211	    Args:
   212	        bam_path: Path to BAM file
   213	        max_reads: Optional limit on number of reads to process
   214	
   215	    Returns:
   216	        Tuple of (read_alignments, fusion_read_ids, fusion_reads)
   217	    """
   218	    logger.info("Processing BAM reads for interval generation...")
   219	
   220	    fusion_read_ids = set()
--- generate intervals ---
   207	def generate_intervals_from_reads(bam_path: str, max_reads: Optional[int] = None) -> Tuple[List[Dict], Set[str], List[Dict]]:
   208	    """
   209	    Generate intervals from BAM read alignments
   210	
   211	    Args:
   212	        bam_path: Path to BAM file
   213	        max_reads: Optional limit on number of reads to process
   214	
   215	    Returns:
   216	        Tuple of (read_alignments, fusion_read_ids, fusion_reads)
   217	    """
   218	    logger.info("Processing BAM reads for interval generation...")
   219	
   220	    fusion_read_ids = set()
   221	    fusion_reads = []
   222	    read_alignments = []
   223	
   224	    with BamReader(bam_path) as bam_reader:
   225	        bam_reader.open()
   226	
   227	        read_count = 0
   228	        skipped_unmapped = 0
   229	        for alignment in bam_reader._alignment_file.fetch(until_eof=True):
   230	            if max_reads is not None and read_count >= max_reads:
   231	                break
   232	
   233	            # Skip unmapped / secondary early. Unmapped reads would otherwise hit
   234	            # get_reference_sequence() -> ValueError on every iteration; in BAMs
   235	            # dominated by unmapped reads this is the hot path. Supplementary
   236	            # alignments are kept because is_fusion_read() depends on them.
   237	            if alignment.is_unmapped or alignment.is_secondary:
   238	                skipped_unmapped += 1
   239	                continue
   240	
   241	            read_dict = bam_reader.alignment_to_dict(alignment)
   242	
   243	            # Check for fusion reads
   244	            # logger.debug(f'Checking if {read_dict["query_name"]} is a fusion-read')
   245	            if is_fusion_read(read_dict):
   246	                logger.debug(f'{read_dict["query_name"]} is a fusion-read, skipping')
   247	                fusion_read_ids.add(read_dict['query_name'])
   248	                fusion_reads.append(read_dict)
   249	                read_count += 1
   250	                if read_count % 10000 == 0:
   251	                    logger.info(f"Processed {read_count} reads...")
   252	                continue
   253	
   254	            # Keep the read alignment info
   255	            read_alignments.append(read_dict)
   256	            read_count += 1
   257	
   258	            if read_count % 10000 == 0:
   259	                logger.info(f"Processed {read_count} reads...")
   260	
   261	    logger.info(
   262	        f"Collected {len(read_alignments)} read alignments, "
   263	        f"{len(fusion_read_ids)} fusion reads ({len(fusion_reads)} fusion read dicts retained), "
   264	        f"skipped {skipped_unmapped} unmapped/secondary"
   265	    )
   266	    return read_alignments, fusion_read_ids, fusion_reads
   267	
   268	
   269	def cluster_intervals(read_alignments: List[Dict], max_gap: int = 0) -> List[GenomicInterval]:
   270	    """
   271	    Cluster overlapping or nearby intervals by chromosome and strand
   272	
   273	    Args:
   274	        read_alignments: List of read alignment dictionaries
   275	        max_gap: Maximum gap between intervals to merge (default: 0bp)
   276	
   277	    Returns:
   278	        List of clustered GenomicInterval objects (strand-separated)
   279	    """
   280	    if not read_alignments:
   281	        return []
   282	
   283	    logger.info(f"Clustering {len(read_alignments)} read alignments with max_gap={max_gap}...")
   284	
   285	    # Group by chromosome and strand
   286	    chrom_strand_intervals = defaultdict(list)
   287	    for read_dict in read_alignments:
   288	        chrom = read_dict.get('reference_name')
   289	        strand = extract_strand_from_read(read_dict) 
   290	        start = read_dict.get('reference_start')
   291	        end = read_dict.get('reference_end')
   292	        read_id = read_dict.get('query_name')
   293	        three_prime_pos = extract_three_prime_pos(read_dict)
   294	
   295	        if chrom and start is not None and end is not None:
   296	            key = (chrom, strand)  # Separate by strand
   297	            chrom_strand_intervals[key].append((start, end, read_id, three_prime_pos))
   298	
   299	    clustered = []
   300	    interval_counter = 0
   301	
   302	    for (chrom, strand), intervals in sorted(chrom_strand_intervals.items()):
   303	        if not intervals:
   304	            continue
   305	
   306	        # Count reads per position for proper read counting
   307	        # position_counts = defaultdict(set)  # position -> set of read_ids
   308	        # for start, end, read_id in intervals:
   309	        #     # Mark all positions in the interval as covered by this read
   310	        #     for pos in range(start, end):
   311	        #         position_counts[pos].add(read_id)
   312	
   313	        # Sort intervals by start position
   314	        intervals_sorted = sorted(intervals, key=lambda x: x[0])
   315	
   316	        current_cluster = None
   317	        current_reads = list()
   318	        current_three_prime_pos = list()
   319	        for start, end, read_id, three_prime_pos in intervals_sorted:
   320	            if current_cluster is None:
--- extract interval reads ---
   576	def extract_reads_for_interval(bam_path: str,
   577	                               interval: GenomicInterval,
   578	                               fusion_read_ids: Optional[Set[str]] = None) -> List[Dict]:
   579	    """
   580	    Extract reads for a specific interval from BAM file
   581	
   582	    Args:
   583	        bam_path: Path to BAM file
   584	        interval: GenomicInterval to extract reads for
   585	        fusion_read_ids: Optional set of fusion read IDs to skip
   586	
   587	    Returns:
   588	        List of read dictionaries
   589	    """
   590	    if fusion_read_ids is None:
   591	        fusion_read_ids = set()
   592	
   593	    reads = []
   594	
   595	    with BamReader(bam_path) as bam_reader:
   596	        bam_reader.open()
   597	
   598	        # Fetch reads from the interval
   599	        for alignment in bam_reader._alignment_file.fetch(
   600	            interval.chrom,
   601	            interval.start,
   602	            interval.end
   603	        ):
   604	            read_dict = bam_reader.alignment_to_dict(alignment)
   605	            read_id = read_dict['query_name']
   606	
   607	            # Skip fusion reads
   608	            if read_id in fusion_read_ids or is_fusion_read(read_dict):
   609	                logger.debug("%s is a fusion-read, skipping", read_id)
   610	                continue
   611	
   612	            # Check if read actually overlaps
   613	            read_start = read_dict.get('reference_start')
   614	            read_end = read_dict.get('reference_end')
   615	
   616	            if (read_start is not None and read_end is not None and
   617	                read_start < interval.end and read_end > interval.start):
   618	                reads.append(read_dict)
   619	
   620	    logger.debug(f"Extracted {len(reads)} reads for interval {interval.region_string}")
   621	    return reads

     1	"""
     2	BAM/SAM file format parser using pysam
     3	"""
     4	
     5	from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Iterator, Tuple, Any, Generator
     6	from pathlib import Path
     7	import logging
     8	
     9	try:
    10	    import pysam
    11	except ImportError:
    12	    raise ImportError(
    13	        "pysam is required for BAM/SAM support. "
    14	        "Install it with: pip install pysam"
    15	    )
    16	
    17	
    18	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19	
    20	
    21	class BamReader:
    22	    """Reader for BAM/SAM alignment files"""
    23	
    24	    def __init__(self, file_path: str, index: bool = True):
    25	        """
    26	        Initialize BAM/SAM reader
    27	
    28	        Args:
    29	            file_path: Path to the BAM/SAM file
    30	            index: Whether to load the index for random access
    31	        """
    32	        self.file_path = Path(file_path)
    33	        if not self.file_path.exists():
    34	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"BAM/SAM file not found: {file_path}")
    35	
    36	        self._alignment_file = None
    37	        self._index = index
    38	        self._is_open = False
    39	
    40	    def __enter__(self):
    41	        """Context manager entry"""
    42	        self.open()
    43	        return self
    44	
    45	    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
    46	        """Context manager exit"""
    47	        self.close()
    48	
    49	    def open(self):
    50	        """Open the alignment file"""
    51	        try:
    52	            mode = 'rb' if self.file_path.suffix.lower() == '.bam' else 'r'
    53	            self._alignment_file = pysam.AlignmentFile(str(self.file_path), mode, index_filename=str(self.file_path) + '.bai' if self._index else None)
    54	            self._is_open = True
    55	
    56	            logger.info(f"Opened alignment file: {self.file_path}")
    57	        except Exception as e:
    58	            logger.error(f"Failed to open alignment file {self.file_path}: {e}")
    59	            raise
    60	
    61	    def close(self):
    62	        """Close the alignment file"""
    63	        if self._alignment_file is not None:
    64	            self._alignment_file.close()
    65	            self._alignment_file = None
    66	            self._is_open = False
    67	            logger.info(f"Closed alignment file: {self.file_path}")
    68	
    69	    @property
    70	    def is_open(self) -> bool:
    71	        """Check if file is open"""
    72	        return self._is_open
    73	
    74	    @property
    75	    def header(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    76	        """
    77	        Get file header
    78	
    79	        Returns:
    80	            Dictionary containing header information
    81	        """
    82	        if self._alignment_file is None:
    83	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
    84	        return dict(self._alignment_file.header)
    85	
    86	    @property
    87	    def references(self) -> List[Tuple[str, int]]:
    88	        """
    89	        Get list of reference sequences
    90	
    91	        Returns:
    92	            List of tuples (reference_name, reference_length)
    93	        """
    94	        if self._alignment_file is None:
    95	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
    96	
    97	        return [(self._alignment_file.get_reference_name(i), self._alignment_file.get_reference_length(i))
    98	                for i in range(self._alignment_file.nreferences)]
    99	
   100	    def count(self, region: Optional[str] = None, read_callback: str = 'all') -> int:
   101	        """
   102	        Count alignments
   103	
   104	        Args:
   105	            region: Region to count (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   106	            read_callback: Read filter (all, mapped, unmapped)
   107	
   108	        Returns:
   109	            Number of alignments
   110	        """
   111	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   112	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   113	
   114	        try:
   115	            return self._alignment_file.count(region=region, read_callback=read_callback)
   116	        except Exception as e:
   117	            logger.error(f"Failed to count alignments: {e}")
   118	            return 0
   119	
   120	    def fetch(self, reference: Optional[str] = None, start: Optional[int] = None,
   121	              end: Optional[int] = None, region: Optional[str] = None,
   122	              until_eof: bool = False) -> Iterator[pysam.AlignedSegment]:
   123	        """
   124	        Fetch alignments from a region
   125	
   126	        Args:
   127	            reference: Reference name
   128	            start: Start position (0-based)
   129	            end: End position
   130	            region: Region string (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   131	            until_eof: Continue to end of file
   132	
   133	        Returns:
   134	            Iterator over AlignedSegment objects
   135	        """
   136	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   137	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   138	
   139	        try:
   140	            # Pass by keyword: pysam.fetch's 5th positional parameter is `tid`,
   141	            # not `until_eof`. Passing until_eof positionally lands it in the tid
   142	            # slot, and tid=False (==0) overrides `contig`, so every region fetch
   143	            # silently returns reads from reference 0 regardless of `reference`.
   144	            return self._alignment_file.fetch(
   145	                contig=reference, start=start, stop=end,
--- io_bam dict ---
   192	    def get_reference_sequence(self, alignment:pysam.AlignedSegment) -> str:
   193	        if alignment.is_secondary:
   194	            return None
   195	        try:
   196	            forward_ref_seq = alignment.get_reference_sequence().upper()
   197	        except ValueError:
   198	            # MD tag not present in BAM (minimap2 without --MD).
   199	            # Downstream consumers default to "" so we can return None safely.
   200	            return None
   201	        return forward_ref_seq if not alignment.is_reverse else self.reverse_complement(forward_ref_seq)
   202	    
   203	    def alignment_to_dict(self, alignment: pysam.AlignedSegment) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   204	        """
   205	        Convert AlignedSegment to dictionary
   206	
   207	        Args:
   208	            alignment: AlignedSegment object
   209	
   210	        Returns:
   211	            Dictionary representation
   212	        """
   213	        if alignment is None:
   214	            return {}
   215	
   216	        return {
   217	            'query_name': alignment.query_name,
   218	            'flag': alignment.flag,
   219	            'is_reverse': alignment.is_reverse,
   220	            'is_secondary': alignment.is_secondary,
   221	            'is_duplicate': alignment.is_duplicate,
   222	            'is_supplementary': alignment.is_supplementary,
   223	            'cigartuples': alignment.cigartuples,
   224	            'reference_name': alignment.reference_name,
   225	            'reference_id': alignment.reference_id,
   226	            'reference_start': alignment.reference_start,
   227	            'reference_end': alignment.reference_end,
   228	            'query_alignment_start': alignment.query_alignment_start,
   229	            'query_alignment_end': alignment.query_alignment_end,
   230	            'query_alignment_length': alignment.query_alignment_length,
   231	            'mapping_quality': alignment.mapping_quality,
   232	            'template_length': alignment.template_length,
   233	            'reference_sequence': self.get_reference_sequence(alignment),
   234	            'is_forward': alignment.is_forward,
   235	            'is_mapped': not alignment.is_unmapped,
   236	            'query_length': alignment.query_length,
   237	            'query_sequence': alignment.query_sequence,
   238	        }
   239	
   240	    def get_reads_in_region(self, region: str, max_reads: Optional[int] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   241	        """
   242	        Get all reads in a specific region
   243	
   244	        Args:
   245	            region: Region string (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   246	            max_reads: Maximum number of reads to return
   247	
   248	        Returns:
   249	            List of read dictionaries
   250	        """
   251	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   252	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   253	
   254	        reads = []
   255	        try:
   256	            for i, alignment in enumerate(self._alignment_file.fetch(region=region)):
   257	                if max_reads is not None and i >= max_reads:
   258	                    break
   259	                reads.append(self.alignment_to_dict(alignment))
   260	        except Exception as e:

   269	def cluster_intervals(read_alignments: List[Dict], max_gap: int = 0) -> List[GenomicInterval]:
   270	    """
   271	    Cluster overlapping or nearby intervals by chromosome and strand
   272	
   273	    Args:
   274	        read_alignments: List of read alignment dictionaries
   275	        max_gap: Maximum gap between intervals to merge (default: 0bp)
   276	
   277	    Returns:
   278	        List of clustered GenomicInterval objects (strand-separated)
   279	    """
   280	    if not read_alignments:
   281	        return []
   282	
   283	    logger.info(f"Clustering {len(read_alignments)} read alignments with max_gap={max_gap}...")
   284	
   285	    # Group by chromosome and strand
   286	    chrom_strand_intervals = defaultdict(list)
   287	    for read_dict in read_alignments:
   288	        chrom = read_dict.get('reference_name')
   289	        strand = extract_strand_from_read(read_dict) 
   290	        start = read_dict.get('reference_start')
   291	        end = read_dict.get('reference_end')
   292	        read_id = read_dict.get('query_name')
   293	        three_prime_pos = extract_three_prime_pos(read_dict)
   294	
   295	        if chrom and start is not None and end is not None:
   296	            key = (chrom, strand)  # Separate by strand
   297	            chrom_strand_intervals[key].append((start, end, read_id, three_prime_pos))
   298	
   299	    clustered = []
   300	    interval_counter = 0
   301	
   302	    for (chrom, strand), intervals in sorted(chrom_strand_intervals.items()):
   303	        if not intervals:
   304	            continue
   305	
   306	        # Count reads per position for proper read counting
   307	        # position_counts = defaultdict(set)  # position -> set of read_ids
   308	        # for start, end, read_id in intervals:
   309	        #     # Mark all positions in the interval as covered by this read
   310	        #     for pos in range(start, end):
   311	        #         position_counts[pos].add(read_id)
   312	
   313	        # Sort intervals by start position
   314	        intervals_sorted = sorted(intervals, key=lambda x: x[0])
   315	
   316	        current_cluster = None
   317	        current_reads = list()
   318	        current_three_prime_pos = list()
   319	        for start, end, read_id, three_prime_pos in intervals_sorted:
   320	            if current_cluster is None:
   321	                # Start new cluster
   322	                current_cluster = (start, end)
   323	                current_reads = [read_id]
   324	                current_three_prime_pos = [three_prime_pos]
   325	            elif start <= current_cluster[1] + max_gap:
   326	                # Merge with current cluster
   327	                current_cluster = (
   328	                    current_cluster[0],
   329	                    max(current_cluster[1], end)
   330	                )
   331	                current_reads.append(read_id)
   332	                current_three_prime_pos.append(three_prime_pos)
   333	            else:
   334	                # Save current cluster and start new one
   335	                clustered.append(GenomicInterval(
   336	                    chrom=chrom,
   337	                    start=current_cluster[0],
   338	                    end=current_cluster[1],
   339	                    strand=strand,
   340	                    read_count=len(current_reads),
   341	                    interval_id=f"interval_{interval_counter:06d}",
   342	                    attrs=current_reads,
   343	                    three_prime_pos=current_three_prime_pos
   344	                    
   345	                ))
   346	                interval_counter += 1
   347	                current_cluster = (start, end)
   348	                current_reads = [read_id]
   349	                current_three_prime_pos = [three_prime_pos]
   350	                
   351	
   352	        # Save the last cluster
   353	        if current_cluster:
   354	            clustered.append(GenomicInterval(
   355	                chrom=chrom,
   356	                start=current_cluster[0],
   357	                end=current_cluster[1],
   358	                strand=strand,
   359	                read_count=len(current_reads),
   360	                interval_id=f"interval_{interval_counter:06d}",
   361	                attrs=current_reads,
   362	                three_prime_pos=current_three_prime_pos
   363	            ))
   364	            interval_counter += 1
   365	
   366	    logger.info(f"Created {len(clustered)} clustered intervals (strand-separated)")
   367	    return clustered
   368

   370	def generate_intervals_from_gtf(gtf_path: str) -> List[GenomicInterval]:
--- isolated intervals ---
   476	def generate_isolated_intervals(bam_path: str,
   477	                                gtf_path: Optional[str] = None,
   478	                                max_gap: int =0,
   479	                                max_reads: Optional[int] = None, tmp_dir: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   480	    """
   481	    Generate isolated, non-overlapping genomic intervals with strand separation
   482	
   483	    This is the main function that generates intervals by analyzing both
   484	    GTF annotation and BAM read alignments. Fusion reads are identified
   485	    and skipped during interval generation.
   486	
   487	    Args:
   488	        bam_path: Path to BAM file (required)
   489	        gtf_path: Path to GTF file (optional, improves annotation)
   490	        max_gap: Maximum gap between intervals to merge (default: 0)
   491	        max_reads: Optional limit on number of reads to process
   492	
   493	    Returns:
   494	        Dictionary with:
   495	        - 'intervals': List of GenomicInterval objects (strand-separated)
   496	        - 'fusion_read_ids': Set of fusion read IDs
   497	        - 'fusion_reads': List of full read dicts for fusion reads
   498	        - 'num_reads_processed': Number of reads processed
   499	    """
   500	    logger.info("=" * 60)
   501	    logger.info("Starting interval generation")
   502	    logger.info(f"BAM: {bam_path}")
   503	    logger.info(f"GTF: {gtf_path}")
   504	    logger.info(f"Max gap: {max_gap}bp")
   505	    logger.info("=" * 60)
   506	
   507	    # Process BAM reads
   508	    read_alignments, fusion_read_ids, fusion_reads = generate_intervals_from_reads(bam_path, max_reads)
   509	
   510	    # Cluster read alignments
   511	    read_intervals = cluster_intervals(read_alignments, max_gap)
   512	    # logger.debug(read_intervals)
   513	    if tmp_dir:
   514	        Path(tmp_dir).mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   515	        tmp_read_intervals = Path(tmp_dir) / "read_intervals.bed"
   516	        # tmp_read_intervals.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   517	        intervals_to_bed(read_intervals,tmp_read_intervals)
   518	    # Generate intervals from GTF if provided
   519	    gtf_intervals = []
   520	    if gtf_path:
   521	        gtf_intervals = generate_intervals_from_gtf(gtf_path)
   522	    if tmp_dir:
   523	        tmp_gtf_intervals = Path(tmp_dir) / "gtf_intervals.bed"
   524	        # tmp_gtf_intervals.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   525	        intervals_to_bed(gtf_intervals,tmp_gtf_intervals)
   526	    # Merge intervals
   527	    final_intervals = merge_gtf_and_read_intervals(
   528	        gtf_intervals,
   529	        read_intervals,
   530	        max_gap=max_gap
   531	    )
   532	    # logger.debug(final_intervals)
   533	    # Sort intervals by chromosome, strand, position
   534	    strand_order = {'+': 0, '-': 1, None: 2}
   535	    final_intervals.sort(key=lambda x: (x.chrom, strand_order.get(x.strand, 2), x.start))
   536	    if tmp_dir:
   537	        tmp_final_intervals = Path(tmp_dir) / "final_intervals.bed"
   538	        # tmp_final_intervals.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   539	        intervals_to_bed(final_intervals,tmp_final_intervals)
   540	    # Log summary
   541	    logger.info("=" * 60)
   542	    logger.info("Interval generation completed")
   543	    logger.info(f"Total intervals: {len(final_intervals)}")
   544	    logger.info(f"Fusion reads detected: {len(fusion_read_ids)}")
   545	
   546	    # Log intervals by strand
   547	    strand_counts = defaultdict(int)
   548	    for interval in final_intervals:
   549	        strand_counts[interval.strand] += 1
   550	
   551	    
   552	    logger.info("Intervals by strand:")
   553	    logger.debug(strand_counts.items())
   554	    for strand, count in sorted(strand_counts.items()):
   555	        logger.info(f"  {strand if strand else 'unstranded'}: {count}")
   556	
   557	    logger.info("=" * 60)
   558	
   559	    # Log first 5 intervals
   560	    logger.info("First 5 intervals:")
   561	    for i, interval in enumerate(final_intervals[:5], 1):
   562	        logger.info(f"  {i}. {interval.region_string} [strand: {interval.strand}] "
   563	                   f"(reads: {interval.read_count})")
   564	
   565	    if len(final_intervals) > 5:
   566	        logger.info(f"  ... and {len(final_intervals) - 5} more intervals")
   567	
   568	    return {
   569	        'intervals': final_intervals,
   570	        'fusion_read_ids': fusion_read_ids,
   571	        'fusion_reads': fusion_reads,
   572	        'num_reads_processed': len(read_alignments) + len(fusion_read_ids)
   573	    }
   574

    21	class BamReader:
    22	    """Reader for BAM/SAM alignment files"""
    23	
    24	    def __init__(self, file_path: str, index: bool = True):
    25	        """
    26	        Initialize BAM/SAM reader
    27	
    28	        Args:
    29	            file_path: Path to the BAM/SAM file
    30	            index: Whether to load the index for random access
    31	        """
    32	        self.file_path = Path(file_path)
    33	        if not self.file_path.exists():
    34	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"BAM/SAM file not found: {file_path}")
    35	
    36	        self._alignment_file = None
    37	        self._index = index
    38	        self._is_open = False
    39	
    40	    def __enter__(self):
    41	        """Context manager entry"""
    42	        self.open()
    43	        return self
    44	
    45	    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
    46	        """Context manager exit"""
    47	        self.close()
    48	
    49	    def open(self):
    50	        """Open the alignment file"""
    51	        try:
    52	            mode = 'rb' if self.file_path.suffix.lower() == '.bam' else 'r'
    53	            self._alignment_file = pysam.AlignmentFile(str(self.file_path), mode, index_filename=str(self.file_path) + '.bai' if self._index else None)
    54	            self._is_open = True
    55	
    56	            logger.info(f"Opened alignment file: {self.file_path}")
    57	        except Exception as e:
    58	            logger.error(f"Failed to open alignment file {self.file_path}: {e}")
    59	            raise
    60	
    61	    def close(self):
    62	        """Close the alignment file"""
    63	        if self._alignment_file is not None:
    64	            self._alignment_file.close()
    65	            self._alignment_file = None
    66	            self._is_open = False
    67	            logger.info(f"Closed alignment file: {self.file_path}")
    68	
    69	    @property
    70	    def is_open(self) -> bool:
    71	        """Check if file is open"""
    72	        return self._is_open
    73	
    74	    @property
    75	    def header(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    76	        """
    77	        Get file header
    78	
    79	        Returns:
    80	            Dictionary containing header information
    81	        """
    82	        if self._alignment_file is None:
    83	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
    84	        return dict(self._alignment_file.header)
    85	
    86	    @property
    87	    def references(self) -> List[Tuple[str, int]]:
    88	        """
    89	        Get list of reference sequences
    90	
    91	        Returns:
    92	            List of tuples (reference_name, reference_length)
    93	        """
    94	        if self._alignment_file is None:
    95	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
    96	
    97	        return [(self._alignment_file.get_reference_name(i), self._alignment_file.get_reference_length(i))
    98	                for i in range(self._alignment_file.nreferences)]
    99	
   100	    def count(self, region: Optional[str] = None, read_callback: str = 'all') -> int:
   101	        """
   102	        Count alignments
   103	
   104	        Args:
   105	            region: Region to count (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   106	            read_callback: Read filter (all, mapped, unmapped)
   107	
   108	        Returns:
   109	            Number of alignments
   110	        """
   111	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   112	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   113	
   114	        try:
   115	            return self._alignment_file.count(region=region, read_callback=read_callback)
   116	        except Exception as e:
   117	            logger.error(f"Failed to count alignments: {e}")
   118	            return 0
   119	
   120	    def fetch(self, reference: Optional[str] = None, start: Optional[int] = None,
   121	              end: Optional[int] = None, region: Optional[str] = None,
   122	              until_eof: bool = False) -> Iterator[pysam.AlignedSegment]:
   123	        """
   124	        Fetch alignments from a region
   125	
   126	        Args:
   127	            reference: Reference name
   128	            start: Start position (0-based)
   129	            end: End position
   130	            region: Region string (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   131	            until_eof: Continue to end of file
   132	
   133	        Returns:
   134	            Iterator over AlignedSegment objects
   135	        """
   136	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   137	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   138	
   139	        try:
   140	            # Pass by keyword: pysam.fetch's 5th positional parameter is `tid`,
   141	            # not `until_eof`. Passing until_eof positionally lands it in the tid
   142	            # slot, and tid=False (==0) overrides `contig`, so every region fetch
   143	            # silently returns reads from reference 0 regardless of `reference`.
   144	            return self._alignment_file.fetch(
   145	                contig=reference, start=start, stop=end,
   146	                region=region, until_eof=until_eof,
   147	            )
   148	        except Exception as e:
   149	            logger.error(f"Failed to fetch alignments: {e}")
   150	            return iter([])
   151
